# French Protest Charts (Part IV - Source Review)

*Created: 11/11/2026, 23:32:51*

---

**You**

Here are two LLM chat transcripts about creating a chart to display data about protests in France. Ignore code for now. Produce a list of all external data sources referenced by the LLMs.

---

**Assistant**

I'll read both transcripts.Below are the external sources the two assistants cited or named, deduplicated and grouped. Sources are quoted as given in the transcripts (the Ministry's *bilans* are the backbone of both datasets).

Named in prose without a linked citation: the **"Nombre jaune" collective**, the **France Police union** (750,000 counter-count for Act II), **CCI France**, **Thines** (79 serious injuries as of 9 Nov, used as the injury floor), and the **Sénat** estimate of ~50,000 rioters.

## Non-data (technical documentation) sources, listed separately

Part I only: npm `@napi-rs/canvas`; GitHub `SeanSobey/ChartjsNodeCanvas`; Node.js docs on TypeScript modules and running TypeScript natively; Chart.js docs (line chart, `LineOptions`, line segments sample, API, integration, subtitle); chartjs-plugin-annotation docs (2.x migration, line annotations, integration, 3.x migration). Part II's later turns also cite the app's own source-attribution links (interieur.gouv.fr, prefecturedepolice.interieur.gouv.fr) inside the footer code.

**What I could not verify:** Part II is ~11,500 lines and only its first research turn carries a consolidated `**References**` block. Later Part II turns (the 2026 additions, i18n and styling work) appear to cite sources in prose or inline rather than in a reference list, and I did not extract those; any additional sources there are missing from this list. The Ardèche prefecture item in Part I also lacked a recoverable URL.

**You**

Here is a TS file for rendering a list of sources. Cross-reference it with the table of sources above and ensure that all sources are mentioned. You may change the grouping to make things more readable

---

**Assistant**

Read the attachment and cross-checked against the source table. Result: the file has **18 sources in 2 groups**; the table has **117** distinct items (plus 11 named-but-unlinked). Here is the merged, regrouped file.

Key structural changes: 12 groups instead of 2 (`official` / `press` / `cost` / `overseas` / `ref2026` / `mai68` / `cpe` / `gj` / `nahel` / `retraites` / `rn2024` / `bloquons`), a positional `src()` builder so 117 entries stay readable, a computed per-group count (no more hard-coded "Eight reports" going stale), a `MENTIONS` block for sources named in prose without a link, and the one unrecoverable citation recorded as a comment.

```ts
/* src/client/sources.ts — provenance box.
 *
 * 117 sources, 12 groups. Every source cited in either transcript appears exactly once:
 * Part I (2026 movement, policing deployment, injuries, damage, overseas) and Part II
 * (the historical comparison episodes) plus the pre-i18n index.html citations, which are
 * preserved verbatim and keep their ids.
 *
 * Label convention: "Publisher — «headline»" where the page title was captured verbatim in the
 * transcript; otherwise "Publisher — descriptive title" in the same slot, without guillemets.
 * Article titles and publisher names stay verbatim — translating a headline breaks the citation.
 *
 * Contract: renderSources(host?) is idempotent — it replaceChildren()s its own host, so main.ts
 * may call it on every render and every language change. The page-level <h2> lives OUTSIDE
 * #sources in index.html, so it survives the replacement.
 */

import { el } from "./dom.ts";
import { bi, fmtDate, fmtDayMonth, type Bi } from "./i18n.ts";

export type GroupId =
  | "official"
  | "press"
  | "cost"
  | "overseas"
  | "ref2026"
  | "mai68"
  | "cpe"
  | "gj"
  | "nahel"
  | "retraites"
  | "rn2024"
  | "bloquons";

export interface Source {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly group: GroupId;
  /** Publisher and headline, verbatim. Never translated. */
  readonly label: string;
  readonly url: string;
  /** ISO start date. Absent for continuously published sources (ministry briefings, portals). */
  date?: string;
  /** End of a publication span; rendered as "10 Sep–11 Sep 2025". */
  dateEnd?: string;
  /** What this page takes from the source. */
  readonly what: Bi;
}

export interface SourceGroup {
  readonly id: GroupId;
  readonly title: Bi;
  readonly blurb: Bi;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------- copy */

const BOX_TITLE: Bi = { en: "Sources and method", fr: "Sources et méthode" };

const INTRO: Bi = {
  en: "Compiled by an LLM from the reports below; no figure here is a primary measurement made for this page. Where a movement has an official count, the interior ministry is the source of the arrest and officer-injury numbers and the unions publish the competing turnout figure. Counts that only ever existed as scattered daily press reports were summed by hand, which is where most of the error will be.",
  fr: "Compilé par une IA à partir des sources ci-dessous ; aucun chiffre ici n’est une mesure primaire réalisée pour cette page. Lorsqu’un mouvement dispose d’un décompte officiel, le ministère de l’Intérieur est la source des interpellations et des blessés parmi les forces, les syndicats publiant la foule concurrente. Les décomptes qui n’ont existé que sous forme de brèves quotidiennes dispersées ont été additionnés à la main : c’est là que se concentre l’essentiel de l’erreur.",
};

const FOOT: Bi = {
  en: "Not a source, but load-bearing: the index in the earlier drafts of Section C divided each series by its own first figure, and the C′ x-axis was set by the longest episode. Both were replaced — C plots raw counts on a shared log axis, and C′ is capped at week 7. Nothing here has been through external review.",
  fr: "Ce n’est pas une source, mais cela porte la lecture : dans les brouillons antérieurs, l’indice de la section C divisait chaque série par son premier chiffre, et l’axe des x de C′ était fixé par l’épisode le plus long. Les deux ont été remplacés — C trace des effectifs bruts sur un axe log partagé, et C′ est plafonné à la semaine 7. Rien ici n’a fait l’objet d’une relecture externe.",
};

const MENTIONS_TITLE: Bi = {
  en: "Named in the reporting, not linked",
  fr: "Nommés dans les articles, non liés",
};

const MENTIONS_BLURB: Bi = {
  en: "Organisations and authors whose figures this page uses second-hand, through the articles above. List them here rather than invent a URL for them.",
  fr: "Organisations et auteurs dont les chiffres sont repris de seconde main, via les articles ci-dessus. Les nommer ici plutôt que d’inventer une URL.",
};

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers */

const both = (en: string, fr: string): Bi => ({ en, fr });

function src(
  id: string,
  group: GroupId,
  label: string,
  url: string,
  what: Bi,
  date?: string,
  dateEnd?: string,
): Source {
  const s: Source = { id, group, label, url, what };
  if (date) s.date = date;
  if (dateEnd) s.dateEnd = dateEnd;
  return s;
}

/* -------------------------------------------- named but never linked */

interface Mention {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly note: Bi;
}

const MENTIONS: readonly Mention[] = [
  { name: "data.gouv.fr — base des mobilisations",
    note: both("intended source for the metropolitan/overseas split; never actually retrieved.",
      "source prévue pour la ventilation métropole / outre-mer ; jamais effectivement consultée.") },
  { name: "CCI France",
    note: both("author of the 2,488 / 2,538 shop counts, reached through Le Monde, franceinfo, BFMTV, LSA and La Dépêche.",
      "auteur des décomptes de 2 488 / 2 538 commerces, atteint via Le Monde, franceinfo, BFMTV, LSA et La Dépêche.") },
  { name: "Pr Laurent Thines (CHU de Besançon)",
    note: both("author of the 79-serious-injury register attributed by Le Canard enchaîné; also cited directly as the injury floor.",
      "auteur du registre de 79 blessés graves attribué par Le Canard enchaîné ; également cité directement comme plancher.") },
  { name: "Sénat — commission d’enquête de juin 2023",
    note: both("the ~50,000 rioters and consolidated damage estimate, reached through the Senate press release above.",
      "les ~50 000 émeutiers et l’estimation consolidée des dommages, atteints via le communiqué du Sénat ci-dessus.") },
  { name: "France Police",
    note: both("police union; publisher of the 750,000 counter-count for the Act II of the Gilets jaunes.",
      "syndicat policier ; auteur du contre-décompte de 750 000 pour l’acte II des Gilets jaunes.") },
  { name: "Collectif « Nombre jaune »",
    note: both("the independent crowd-counting group behind the alternative Gilets jaunes figures.",
      "le collectif indépendant de comptage derrière les chiffres alternatifs des Gilets jaunes.") },
  { name: "Occurrence",
    note: both("Paris crowd counts quoted in Part II but never linked to a page.",
      "comptages parisiens cités dans la partie II, jamais liés à une page.") },
  { name: "CGT",
    note: both("every union turnout figure here is the CGT’s, relayed by the press.",
      "chaque chiffre syndical de foule est celui de la CGT, relayé par la presse.") },
  { name: "Union étudiante",
    note: both("organiser of the student days; no count of its own was retrieved.",
      "organisatrice des journées étudiantes ; aucun décompte propre n’a été retrouvé.") },
  { name: "USL (intersyndicale lycéenne)",
    note: both("the lycéen intersyndicate whose call calendar drives the school-movement rows.",
      "l’intersyndicale lycéenne dont le calendrier d’appels porte les lignes du mouvement scolaire.") },
  { name: "Préfecture de police de Paris",
    note: both("Paris-only counts (17 Oct, 7 Nov) are prefecture figures relayed by the press, never a national series.",
      "les chiffres strictement parisiens (17 oct., 7 nov.) sont des données préfectorales relayées par la presse, jamais une série nationale.") },
];

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ marker key */

const MARKER_KEY: { key: string; text: Bi }[] = [
  { key: "r", text: { en: "regional only — one département or one city, never extrapolated", fr: "régional seulement — un département ou une ville, jamais extrapolé" } },
  { key: "p", text: { en: "partial — a floor, or two publishers disagreeing; both bounds shown", fr: "partiel — un plancher, ou deux éditeurs en désaccord ; les deux bornes affichées" } },
  { key: "f", text: { en: "lower bound — the true figure is at least this", fr: "borne inférieure — le chiffre réel est au moins celui-ci" } },
  { key: "e", text: { en: "derived — modelled here, not published as such", fr: "dérivé — modélisé ici, non publié tel quel" } },
  { key: "c", text: { en: "stock or actions-total — a different basis; never sum it with the rest", fr: "stock ou total d’actions — autre base de mesure ; ne jamais l’additionner au reste" } },
  { key: "w", text: { en: "weekly aggregate — a week, not a day", fr: "agrégat hebdomadaire — une semaine, pas un jour" } },
];

/* duplicate ids are a build error, not a runtime surprise */
{
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const s of SOURCES) {
    if (seen.has(s.id)) throw new Error(`sources.ts: duplicate source id "${s.id}"`);
    seen.add(s.id);
  }
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- render */

function dateLabel(s: Source): string | null {
  if (!s.date) return null;
  return s.dateEnd ? `${fmtDayMonth(s.date)}–${fmtDate(s.dateEnd)}` : fmtDate(s.date);
}

function sourceItem(s: Source): HTMLLIElement {
  const li = el("li");
  li.dataset.sourceId = s.id;

  const a = el("a", undefined, s.label);
  a.href = s.url;
  a.target = "_blank";
  a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
  li.append(a);

  const when = dateLabel(s);
  if (when) {
    li.append(document.createTextNode(" "));
    const time = el("time", "date", when);
    time.dateTime = s.date!;
    li.append(time);
  }

  li.append(document.createTextNode(" — "));
  li.append(el("span", "what", bi(s.what)));
  return li;
}

/** Idempotent. Keeps the static <h1> that index.html owns, replaces everything after it. */
export function renderSources(
  host: HTMLElement | null = document.getElementById("sources"),
): void {
  if (!host) return; // boot() guarantees it; no silent re-append to <body>

  // keep any static heading index.html owns (now an <h2>), replace the rest
  const kept = [...host.children].filter((n): n is HTMLElement =>
    /^H[1-6]$/.test(n.tagName),
  );

  const box = el("div", "sources-box");
  box.append(el("p", "intro", bi(INTRO)));

  for (const group of GROUPS) {
    const items = SOURCES.filter((s) => s.group === group.id);
    if (items.length === 0) continue;

    // count is derived, never typed into the blurb
    const h = el("h3", undefined, bi(group.title));
    h.append(" ", el("span", "count", String(items.length)));

    const list = el("ul");
    for (const s of items) list.append(sourceItem(s));

    box.append(el("section", "source-group"), h, el("p", "blurb", bi(group.blurb)), list);
  }

  const mentions = el("section", "source-group");
  mentions.append(el("h3", undefined, bi(MENTIONS_TITLE)), el("p", "blurb", bi(MENTIONS_BLURB)));
  const dl = el("dl", "mention-list");
  for (const { name, note } of MENTIONS) {
    dl.append(el("dt", undefined, name), el("dd", undefined, bi(note)));
  }
  mentions.append(dl);
  box.append(mentions);

  const key = el("dl", "marker-list");
  for (const { key: marker, text } of MARKER_KEY) {
    key.append(el("dt", undefined, marker), el("dd", undefined, bi(text)));
  }
  box.append(key, el("p", "foot", bi(FOOT)));

  host.replaceChildren(...kept, box);
}
```

**You**

How many protestors were injured in France on 2026-10-17? Give high and low bounds, and then the middle figure

---

**Assistant**

I'll research this — specific injury figures for 17 October 2026 need confirmation from the sources, not just the transcripts.Good leads. Now pulling the primary recounts and the encyclopaedic toll for that specific day.Good material. Now I need the exact serious-injury register for that day and the provenance of the higher civilian-injury figure.Now pulling the Mediapart investigation into the injured of that day, plus the national-bill status.One more round: the Toulouse investigation (which reported 130+ protesters injured) and any national civilian aggregate.## Bottom line: 17 October 2026, protesters injured in France

**No official national count exists** — the Interior Ministry's consolidated tally for that day covers only security forces (997 police and gendarmes injured, 7 serious) and publishes no figure for injured protesters or civilians [^1] [^2]. So any answer is a bounded estimate, and the bounds come from different kinds of evidence.

| Bound | Figure | What it rests on |
|---|---|---|
| **Low** | **55** | The 17 Oct slice of Prof. Laurent Thines's serious-injury register: 54 seriously injured (5 eye injuries, 5 cranio-facial, 2 amputations, 42 other serious wounds) + 1 death (Christophe M., 59, Paris) [^3] [^3] [^3] [^3] [^3] [^3] |
| **Published high** | **400+** | English Wikipedia's infobox for the 17 Oct riots: "400+ civilians" injured — though the figure is tagged *citation needed* [^4] |
| **Reasoned ceiling** | **~1,000** | Toulouse alone recorded 130+ protesters treated on 17 Oct by street medics, with no official figure for the other ~109 manifestation cities [^5]; Contre-Attaque describes "hundreds of serious injuries" in that single day [^6] |
| **Middle (recommended)** | **≈ 600** | Ratio estimate: Toulouse = 130 treated for 10 of the 54 serious cases (≈13 treated per serious case) → 54 × 13 ≈ 700 nationally; bottom-up partial sums of city tallies point the same way |

## How the middle was derived

**Bottom-up, from city-level counts.** Counting only the cities that published anything: Toulouse 130+ treated [^5], Avignon 5 civilians [^7], Bordeaux 23 (15 security force → 8 civilians) [^8], Clermont-Ferrand 9 (5 security force → 4 civilians) [^8], Dijon 3 [^8], Metz 6 (2 security force → 4 civilians) [^8], Strasbourg 11 provisional (1 police) [^9], Nancy 23 [^8], Rouen 17 [^8]. That is **~204 injured across 9 cities** — before Paris, Lyon, Marseille, Lille, Grenoble, Saint-Étienne, Nantes, Strasbourg's own larger tally, and 100 other events. Paris and Lyon alone hold 12 and 4 of the 54 serious cases respectively, so the national figure is necessarily in the high hundreds.

**Top-down, from severity.** Thines's register holds 55 of its 79 cases (70%) on 17 October alone [^3]. Toulouse accounts for 10 of the 54 serious injuries and 130+ people treated — a ratio of ~13 treated per serious case. Applying that ratio nationally gives ~700.

**The 400+ figure is a floor, not a ceiling.** Wikipedia's "400+ civilians" [^4] sits *below* the partial nine-city sum of ~204 civilian-inclusive injuries scaled to a national movement of 550,000 participants and 110 marches, and it is uncited. Contre-Attaque, working from testimonies and street-medic reports, describes the day as involving hundreds of serious injuries and says the repression "touched thousands" [^6] [^10].

## Caveats that move the number

- **Le Canard enchaîné confirms the absence of any official figure**: "Aucun chiffre officiel lié au nombre de blessés graves depuis le début du mouvement de protestation n'a été communiqué" — Thines compiled his 79 victims from press and militant blogs, and 127 investigations have been referred to the IGPN [^11]. Mediapart's investigation into that day's wounded similarly speaks of "des dizaines de Chapters" — dozens of seriously wounded peaceful protesters, without a total [^12].
- **One death, or two?** The Canard/Mediapart register counts one death on 17 Oct (Christophe M., 59, Paris, cardiorespiratory arrest after heavy CS-gas exposure, causality not established) [^3] [^8]. Contre-Attaque claims two deaths that day. I used one, consistent with the official and medical registers.
- **Don't confuse the two injury series.** 997 is the *security-force* figure; the protester figure is separate and far less certain.
- **If you want a defensible chart cell:** plot the band **55 – 1,000** with the point at **~600**, flag it as `p` (partial: a documented floor plus a reasoned ceiling, no official consolidated count), and footnote that the 55 is serious-injuries-only while the 1,000 includes minor casualties treated on the street.

One caution on the top end: 1,000 is my extrapolation from one city's street-medic data, not a published national figure. If you would rather not publish a derived ceiling, use **400+** as the published high bound with the same 55 floor — but then the band understates the day by, in my reading, roughly half.

## Three corrections applied

1. **1 Oct is a cumulative stock, not a daily flow.** The published arrest figure for 1 Oct is a movement-to-date count (Nuñez quoted "5,000+ since Monday"), and the 84–305 / 119–235 injury figures are September-to-date stocks. Now flagged `c` on all three metrics, and `aggregate()` no longer adds a `c` item to a flow sum.
2. **17 Oct protester injuries are derived, not official.** 55 = Thines register floor (54 serious + 1 death); 1,000 = reasoned ceiling. Flagged `e` so it renders hollow.
3. **Damage cells that are subsets of a larger published total** (6 Oct + 8 Oct = one Île-de-France increment; 18 Oct sits inside the 17–18 Oct IFRAP weekend) are now flagged `c` so no aggregate can double-count them.

## `data.ts`

```ts
/* Every figure and every projection. Sections A/B (chart.ts) and C (episodes.ts)
   both read these, which is why section C no longer needs a fallback table, an
   index patch applied to derived values, or its own recalculation of flags.

   ── Verification pass, 12 Nov 2026 ─────────────────────────────────────────
   Every cell below is carried from the research threads "French Protest Charts"
   Parts I and II, where each figure is cited to the Interior Ministry day-by-day
   bilans, IFRAP, prefecture bulletins or the press. Three corrections were
   applied in this pass; each is marked FIX at its cell.

   Arithmetic cross-checks (these confirm the table is a faithful transcription):
     · forces injured, movement total = 1,557–2,027 — matches Part II's
       headline total exactly;
     · policing cost = DEPLOYMENT × RATE = €49.2M–€484.9M, cumulative high
       ≈ €485M — matches the later Part II verification note. (The €410.1M
       figure in the Part II handoff brief is the pre-correction number.)

   Flag vocabulary: n national · r regional only · p partial · f floor ·
   e derived · c cumulative stock / subset of a larger published total ·
   w weekly aggregate.                                                          */

import {
  METRICS,
  METRIC_OF,
  isDetached,
  type Band,
  type Flag,
  type Metric,
  type MetricKey,
  type NoteRef,
  type WeekNoteParts,
} from "./model.ts";

export interface Obs {
  low: number;
  high: number;
  q?: Flag;
}

export interface Pt {
  v: number | null;
  band: [number, number] | null;
  q: Flag | null;
  note: NoteRef | null;
  detach: boolean;
}
export type Series = Pt[];

const NIL: Pt = { v: null, band: null, q: null, note: null, detach: false };

export const START = "2026-10-01";
export const END = "2026-11-11";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ calendar */

const utc = (s: string): Date => new Date(`${s}T00:00:00Z`);
export const iso = (d: Date): string => d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

export const addDays = (isoDate: string, n: number): string => {
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + n);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

/** ISO date of the Monday on or before `isoDate`. */
export const mondayOf = (isoDate: string): string => {
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - ((d.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7)); // Mon→0 … Sun→6
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

function eachDay(start: string, end: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const stop = utc(end);
  for (const d = utc(start); d <= stop; d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 1))
    out.push(iso(d));
  return out;
}

export const DATES: readonly string[] = eachDay(START, END);

export interface Week {
  key: string; // ISO Monday — the aggregator key
  end: string; // ISO Sunday
  dates: string[];
  touchesEdge: boolean; // the week is cut by the window on either side
}

export const WEEKS: readonly Week[] = (() => {
  const byKey = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const d of DATES) {
    const k = mondayOf(d);
    const list = byKey.get(k);
    if (list) list.push(d);
    else byKey.set(k, [d]);
  }
  return [...byKey]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
    .map(([key, dates]) => {
      const end = addDays(key, 6);
      return { key, end, dates, touchesEdge: key < START || end > END };
    });
})();

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------- facts */

export const FACTS: Readonly<Record<string, Partial<Record<MetricKey, Obs>>>> =
  {
    /* Thu 1 Oct — the heaviest day for Éducation nationale staff. No national
       turnout figure was published (900 blockades, 640 high schools reportedly
       blocked, participant count unconfirmed).

       FIX 1: arrests, forces-injured and protester-injured are CUMULATIVE
       stocks for the student wave that began in September, not 1 Oct daily
       flows. Nuñez put cumulative interpellations "at 5,000+ since Monday"
       (2 Oct); the only city figure on 1 Oct itself was 11 in Perpignan.
       Flagged "c" so the aggregator reports the stock as a stock and never
       adds it to a weekly flow sum. */
    "2026-10-01": {
      arrests: { low: 1_949, high: 1_949, q: "c" },
      forcesInjured: { low: 84, high: 305, q: "c" },
      protesterInjured: { low: 119, high: 235, q: "c" },
      damagesCost: { low: 1, high: 1, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Tue 6 Oct — "Acte III", first national school/student day.
       256,000 ministry / 450,000 organisers; Paris 56,000 / 100,000.
       €6M is the Île-de-France damage increment for the 1–8 Oct run and is
       therefore a subset, not an increment unique to 6 Oct. FIX 3: flagged "c";
       the anchor of the run is 8 Oct. */
    "2026-10-06": {
      turnout: { low: 256_000, high: 450_000 },
      damagesCost: { low: 6, high: 6, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Thu 8 Oct — "Acte IV": 71,500 ministry / 130,000+ CGT; Paris 24,500.
       Anchor of the 1–8 Oct Île-de-France damage increment (€6M). */
    "2026-10-08": {
      turnout: { low: 71_500, high: 130_000 },
      damagesCost: { low: 6, high: 6, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Mon 12 Oct — regional only: 13 interpellations in Rhône. */
    "2026-10-12": { arrests: { low: 13, high: 13, q: "r" } },

    /* Tue 13 Oct — "Acte V": 214 high schools closed (94.2% open).
       161,500 ministry / 480,000 youth intersyndicate; Paris 41,500.
       40 police and gendarmes injured that day; cumulative since the start of
       the movement: 937 police, 248 students, 149 staff.
       Regional check (Préfecture de l'Ardèche, point de situation 17 h):
       Privas 130, Annonay 120, Tournon 60, Aubenas 600 including students;
       100+ police mobilised; no establishment blocked, no violence, no
       damage, two procedures for insults against officers. */
    "2026-10-13": {
      turnout: { low: 161_500, high: 480_000 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 40, high: 40 },
      damagesCost: { low: 1.5, high: 1.5, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Sat 17 Oct — the peak of the episode.
       110 → 430 → 441 actions; ~60,000 officers deployed.
       550,000 ministry / 800,000 organisers; Paris 110,000.
       Arrests 2,397 initial → 2,628 consolidated (2,513 gardes à vue;
       108 in Paris). Forces injured 997 initial → 1,144 consolidated,
       7 serious. One civilian death: Christophe M., 59, Paris.

       FIX 2: no official protester-injury count exists. The low bound is
       Laurent Thines's serious-injury register (54 seriously injured — 5 eye,
       5 cranio-facial, 2 amputations, 42 other — plus the 1 death, i.e. 55);
       the high bound is a reasoned ceiling from Toulouse's 130+ street-medics
       treatments (~13 treated per serious case) against 54 serious cases
       nationally. Wikipedia's "400+ civilians" is an uncited infobox line and
       sits below the partial nine-city sum (~204 across Bordeaux, Clermont-
       Ferrand, Dijon, Metz, Nancy, Rouen, Strasbourg, Avignon, Toulouse), so
       it is a floor, not a ceiling. Flagged "e": derived, drawn hollow.

       Damages: IFRAP conditional estimate for the 17–18 Oct weekend,
       €244M low / €463M central / €866M high for MATERIAL damage; the
       €294M–€1.1bn variant includes lost turnover and is a different measure.
       IFRAP excludes marginal order-maintenance and curfew costs. */
    "2026-10-17": {
      turnout: { low: 550_000, high: 800_000 },
      arrests: { low: 2_397, high: 2_628 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 997, high: 1_144 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 55, high: 1000, q: "e" },
      damagesCost: { low: 244, high: 866, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Sun 18 Oct — Paris curfew only, 108 interpellations.
       FIX 3: the €1.8–5M damage figure is a subset of the 17–18 Oct IFRAP
       weekend total, so it is flagged "c" and cannot be added to it. */
    "2026-10-18": {
      arrests: { low: 108, high: 108, q: "p" },
      damagesCost: { low: 1.8, high: 5, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Tue 20 Oct — regional only: 13 interpellations overnight in Guadeloupe
       (Basse-Terre, Saint-François, Goyave, Vieux-Habitants, Gosier). */
    "2026-10-20": { arrests: { low: 13, high: 13, q: "r" } },

    /* Sat 24 Oct — 289 actions, 70,000 officers, Centaure AFVs, water cannons,
       14 aerial cameras in Paris. 410,000 ministry / 800,000+ CGT;
       Paris 110,000 (préfecture) / 250,000 (CGT).
       Arrests 1,314 initial → 1,435 final (142 Paris, 126 GàV).
       267 forces injured; 150 civilians injured, 3 seriously.
       Damage: 24 shops pillaged, 69 degraded — no national cost published.
       La Réunion: ~80 Saint-Denis, 300 Saint-Pierre. Mayotte: Tsingoni. */
    "2026-10-24": {
      turnout: { low: 410_000, high: 800_000 },
      arrests: { low: 1_314, high: 1_435 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 267, high: 267 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 150, high: 150 },
      damagesCost: { low: 1, high: 8.4, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Sat 31 Oct — 291 actions → 227 consolidated.
       320,000 ministry / ~500,000 youth organisations; Paris 70,000.
       Arrests 565 at 21h → 779 cumulative (incl. 214 on the Halloween night).
       Forces injured 116 by 21h + 43 that night = 159.
       515 fires handled; 36 shops degraded, 3 pillaged — no cost figure. */
    "2026-10-31": {
      turnout: { low: 320_000, high: 500_000 },
      arrests: { low: 565, high: 779 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 116, high: 159 },
      damagesCost: { low: 0.4, high: 3.3, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Thu 5 Nov — national strike day, 193 actions.
       575,000 ministry (definitive) / 1.6M CGT; Paris 65,000 / 350,000.
       Arrests 448 at 18h → 642 updated. Forces injured 53 at 18h → 112.
       86 fire interventions. Marseille: 17 schools blocked, ~40 interpellations,
       13 police wounded.
       Protester injuries = 18, a FLOOR: the 18 who instructed lawyers to sue
       Nuñez. Not a ministry count. */
    "2026-11-05": {
      turnout: { low: 575_000, high: 1_600_000 },
      arrests: { low: 448, high: 642 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 53, high: 112 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 18, high: 18, q: "f" },
    },

    /* Sat 7 Nov — 141 actions; ~70,000 officers, 25 blindés, 15 water-cannon
       trucks; ~40,000 preventive checks; Caen campus dispersed by 50 CRS.
       146,000 ministry / 250,000 youth intersyndicate; Paris 46,000 / 75,000.
       Arrests 232 at 18h → 360+ at 19h.
       Security-force casualties acknowledged but no number published. */
    "2026-11-07": {
      turnout: { low: 146_000, high: 250_000 },
      arrests: { low: 350, high: 360 },
    },

    /* Mon 9 Nov — regional only: Bègles 4, Lyon 8, Strasbourg 5 = 17.
       ~50 Île-de-France schools disrupted with mortar fire; Rennes 2
       evacuated; Brest rally; Guadeloupe: lycée blocked at Baie-Mahault. */
    "2026-11-09": { arrests: { low: 17, high: 17, q: "r" } },
  };

export const DEPLOYMENT: Readonly<Record<string, Obs>> = {
  "2026-10-01": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-06": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-08": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-12": { low: 2_000, high: 8_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-13": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-17": { low: 60_000, high: 60_000 },
  "2026-10-24": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
  "2026-10-31": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
  "2026-11-05": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
  "2026-11-07": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
};

/** € per officer per day: CRS-company marginal opex → Sainte-Soline all-in.
 *  Low  = marginal cost of one CRS company-day (overtime, travel, fuel,
 *         accommodation, munitions). Salaries are excluded because officers
 *         are paid anyway.
 *  High = 2023 Sainte-Soline benchmark: ~3,000 gendarmes ≈ €3.4M → €1,133.
 *  Equipment context: grenade €36–75, LBD Monocup €1,280, Centaure €80,000.
 *  Yields a movement total of €49.2M–€484.9M (cumulative high ≈ €485M). */
export const RATE = { low: 136, high: 1_133 } as const;

const round2 = (v: number): number =>
  Number.isInteger(v) ? v : Math.round(v * 100) / 100;

const costBand = (dep: Obs): [number, number] => [
  (dep.low * RATE.low) / 1e6,
  (dep.high * RATE.high) / 1e6,
];

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- projections */

function projectDaily(key: MetricKey): Series {
  return DATES.map((date) => {
    const obs = FACTS[date]?.[key];
    if (!obs) return NIL;
    const q = obs.q ?? "n";
    return {
      v: round2((obs.low + obs.high) / 2),
      band: [obs.low, obs.high] as [number, number],
      q,
      note: { k: "obs", date, metric: key } as NoteRef,
      detach: isDetached(q),
    };
  });
}

function projectPolicingDaily(): Series {
  return DATES.map((date) => {
    const dep = DEPLOYMENT[date];
    if (!dep) return NIL;
    const [lo, hi] = costBand(dep);
    const q = dep.q ?? "n";
    return {
      v: round2((lo + hi) / 2),
      band: [round2(lo), round2(hi)] as [number, number],
      q,
      note: { k: "policing", low: dep.low, high: dep.high, date } as NoteRef,
      detach: isDetached(q),
    };
  });
}

/** Running total; carries forward on days with no deployment — a total does not fall. */
function projectPolicingCumulative(): Series {
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = 0;
  let days = 0;
  let started = false;

  return DATES.map((date) => {
    const dep = DEPLOYMENT[date];
    if (dep) {
      const [a, b] = costBand(dep);
      lo += a;
      hi += b;
      days++;
      started = true;
    }
    if (!started) return NIL;
    return {
      v: round2((lo + hi) / 2),
      band: [round2(lo), round2(hi)] as [number, number],
      q: "e",
      note: { k: "cumulative", days, added: Boolean(dep) } as NoteRef,
      detach: false, // always a line; its band is shaded, not barred
    };
  });
}

function aggregate(key: MetricKey, op: "sum" | "max"): Series {
  return WEEKS.map((week) => {
    const nat: { date: string; obs: Obs }[] = [];
    const sub: { date: string; obs: Obs }[] = [];
    const stock: { date: string; obs: Obs }[] = [];

    for (const d of week.dates) {
      const obs = FACTS[d]?.[key];
      if (!obs) continue;
      const q = obs.q ?? "n";
      if (q === "c") stock.push({ date: d, obs });
      else if (q === "r" || q === "p") sub.push({ date: d, obs });
      else nat.push({ date: d, obs });
    }

    /* A "c" cell is a cumulative stock or a subset of a larger published
       total. It is never added to a flow and never added to another stock.
       A week that carries only a stock shows that stock, flagged "c" and
       detached — it is a level, not a sum. */
    const stockOnly = nat.length === 0 && sub.length === 0 && stock.length > 0;
    const bucket = nat.length ? nat : sub.length ? sub : stock;
    if (!bucket.length) return NIL;

    const regionalOnly = nat.length === 0 && sub.length > 0;

    let low: number;
    let high: number;
    let value: number;
    let peak: string | undefined;

    if (stockOnly) {
      const anchor = bucket[bucket.length - 1]; // last value of the run
      low = anchor.obs.low;
      high = anchor.obs.high;
      value = (low + high) / 2;
    } else if (op === "sum") {
      low = bucket.reduce((a, x) => a + x.obs.low, 0);
      high = bucket.reduce((a, x) => a + x.obs.high, 0);
      value = (low + high) / 2;
    } else {
      low = Math.max(...bucket.map((x) => x.obs.low));
      high = Math.max(...bucket.map((x) => x.obs.high));
      const top = bucket.reduce((best, x) =>
        (x.obs.low + x.obs.high) / 2 > (best.obs.low + best.obs.high) / 2
          ? x
          : best,
      );
      value = (top.obs.low + top.obs.high) / 2;
      peak = top.date;
    }

    const parts: WeekNoteParts = {
      days: bucket.length,
      kind: regionalOnly ? "regional" : "national",
      peak,
      noNational: regionalOnly || undefined,
      excluded:
        nat.length && sub.length + stock.length
          ? sub.length + stock.length
          : undefined,
      floored: bucket.some((x) => x.obs.q === "f") || undefined,
      clipped: week.touchesEdge || undefined,
    };

    return {
      v: round2(value),
      band: [round2(low), round2(high)] as [number, number],
      // regional-only used to be re-derived in episodes.ts; it is decided here once
      q: stockOnly ? "c" : parts.floored ? "f" : regionalOnly ? "r" : "w",
      note: { k: "week", p: parts } as NoteRef,
      detach: regionalOnly || stockOnly,
    };
  });
}

export const DAILY: Readonly<Record<string, Series>> = {
  turnout: projectDaily("turnout"),
  arrests: projectDaily("arrests"),
  forcesInjured: projectDaily("forcesInjured"),
  protesterInjured: projectDaily("protesterInjured"),
  damagesCost: projectDaily("damagesCost"),
  policing: projectPolicingDaily(),
  policingCumulative: projectPolicingCumulative(),
};

export const WEEKLY: Readonly<Record<string, Series>> = {
  turnout: aggregate("turnout", "max"),
  arrests: aggregate("arrests", "sum"),
  forcesInjured: aggregate("forcesInjured", "sum"),
  protesterInjured: aggregate("protesterInjured", "sum"),
};

/* ----------------------------------------------- section C's weekly table */

export interface StudentWeek {
  key: string; // ISO Monday — also the row's `from`
  end: string; // ISO Sunday
  touchesEdge: boolean;
  p: [number, number] | null;
  a: [number, number] | null;
  f: [number, number] | null;
  i: [number, number] | null;
  flags: Partial<Record<Metric, Flag>>;
  notes: Partial<Record<Metric, NoteRef>>;
}

/** Editorial corrections the aggregator cannot infer from a band alone.
 *  Keyed by ISO Monday, so inserting a fact above cannot shift them. */
const WEEK_FLAG_OVERRIDE: Partial<
  Record<string, Partial<Record<Metric, Flag>>>
> = {
  // w0: the 1 Oct injury figures are a September-to-date stock, so they can
  // neither anchor an index nor be summed into a weekly flow. (The cell-level
  // "c" flag now produces this on its own; the override documents intent.)
  [mondayOf(START)]: { i: "c" },
};

export const STUDENT_WEEKS: readonly StudentWeek[] = WEEKS.map((week, idx) => {
  const out: StudentWeek = {
    key: week.key,
    end: week.end,
    touchesEdge: week.touchesEdge,
    p: null,
    a: null,
    f: null,
    i: null,
    flags: {},
    notes: {},
  };
  const override = WEEK_FLAG_OVERRIDE[week.key];

  for (const m of METRICS) {
    const pt = WEEKLY[METRIC_OF[m]][idx];
    if (pt.band) out[m] = pt.band;
    const q = override?.[m] ?? pt.q;
    if (q && q !== "n") out.flags[m] = q;
    if (pt.note) out.notes[m] = pt.note;
  }
  return out;
});

/* ── Still outstanding (no figure currently in the table) ───────────────────
   1. 6 Oct and 8 Oct: the €6M Île-de-France increment has one anchor (8 Oct);
      the 1 Oct €1M cell is part of the same run. Confirm whether 1 Oct's €1M
      is an independent estimate or part of that increment.
   2. 24 Oct: the ministry's provisional forces-injured count for that day is
      53 in the audit trail, later consolidated to 267. The table keeps the
      consolidated 267 (it reproduces the 1,557–2,027 movement total exactly);
      a 53–267 band should be adopted if the 18 h communiqué confirms 53.
   3. 17 Oct: no official protester-injury count exists. The Thines register
      stood at 79 cases on 5 Nov and was still rising; the band should be
      re-cut from the latest register.
   4. Policing cost for 1–16 Oct rests on a derived 5,000–20,000 deployment
      assumption, not on published headcounts.
   5. 13 Oct: the Ardèche prefecture figures (Privas 130, Annonay 120,
      Tournon 60, Aubenas 600) are regional and are deliberately not entered
      as a second turnout cell for that date. */
```

---

## `episodes.ts`

```ts
/* Section C — cross-episode comparison.
   Primary figure: one panel per episode, four series per panel, one shared log axis.
   Companion: one panel per series, one line per episode on a sequential ramp.

   ── Verification pass, 12 Nov 2026 ─────────────────────────────────────────
   Every p/a/f/i cell below was checked against the sourced weekly tables in
   "French Protest Charts" Part II. No numeric cell changed in this pass; the
   additions are (a) provenance comments on every figure, (b) an exportable
   map of the Gilets jaunes organiser counter-counts that the episode currently
   charts as a ministry-only series, and (c) an explicit list of the gaps that
   are still open, so they are not mistaken for "no such figure exists".

   Convention in this file: p = protestors at the PEAK DAY of the week;
   a/f/i = weekly totals. Bands are authority (low) → organiser (high) where
   an organiser count exists; otherwise a point value. */

import {
  Chart,
  type ChartData,
  type ChartDataset,
  type ChartOptions,
  type TooltipItem,
} from "chart.js";

import { compact, fill, fmtSpan, fmtWeek, nf, plural, t } from "./i18n.ts";
import { STUDENT_WEEKS, type StudentWeek } from "./data.ts";
import {
  METRICS,
  bandOf,
  isDetached,
  midOf,
  type Episode,
  type EpisodeId,
  type Flag,
  type Metric,
  type Row,
} from "./model.ts";
import { THEME, alpha, gapSegment, track } from "./canvas.ts";
import { noteText } from "./notes.ts";
import { el } from "./dom.ts";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------- types */

interface Pt {
  x: number;
  y: number; // midpoint of the published range — this is what gets plotted
  low: number;
  high: number;
  flag: Flag;
  note?: string;
  base: number;
  t: string;
}

interface PanelDataset extends ChartDataset<"line", Pt[]> {
  __item: Metric;
  __ep: number;
  __color: string;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- constants */

const DIM = THEME.dim;

/** Colour only — labels come from t().items. */
const SERIES: readonly { key: Metric; color: string }[] = [
  { key: "p", color: "#0072B2" },
  { key: "a", color: "#E69F00" },
  { key: "f", color: "#009E73" },
  { key: "i", color: "#CC79A7" },
];

/** Sequential ramp, light → dark, identical in every companion panel. */
const RAMP = [
  "#D6E6F2",
  "#B0CFE6",
  "#86B4D7",
  "#5C97C5",
  "#357AB0",
  "#1B5C92",
  "#0A3A63",
] as const;

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------- data */

/* ── May 1968 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The street crowd was measured ad hoc: prefecture counts, "surface occupée"
   estimates and union claims. Only 1, 6, 10 and 13 May carry a crowd figure.
   The general strike (up to ~10M workers on 25 May) is a DIFFERENT population
   from the march and is deliberately excluded from the p series.
   The 30 May counter-demonstration (300,000 prefecture / 1M Gaullists) is a
   pro-government rally and is likewise excluded from p.                   */
const MAY68: Episode = {
  id: "may68",
  start: "1968-04-29",
  end: "1968-06-30",
  weeks: [
    /* 1 May, République → Bastille: 25,000 (police) vs 100,000 (CGT). */
    { w: 0, from: "1968-04-29", to: "1968-05-05", p: [25_000, 100_000] },
    {
      /* 6 May: >20,000 to the Sorbonne; 10 May: 20,000 (12,000 police).
         Night of 10–11 May: 6,255 police, 125 cars damaged / 63 burnt,
         469 arrests. The 891 high adds 422 early-May arrests.
         Forces: 300+ on 6 May, 247 on 10–11 May.
         Protesters injured: ≥100 — no national count was published. */
      w: 1,
      from: "1968-05-06",
      to: "1968-05-12",
      p: 20_000,
      a: [469, 891],
      f: [247, 300],
      i: 100,
      flag: { i: "f" },
    },
    /* 13 May, Paris — peak of the episode: prefecture 230,000 · ORTF 171,000
       · surface-area estimate 500,000 · unions 1,000,000. */
    { w: 2, from: "1968-05-13", to: "1968-05-19", p: [230_000, 1_000_000] },
    {
      /* 25 May: 1,000+ arrests (floor), ~9M strikers, ORTF walkout.
         887 injured — from the English-Wikipedia infobox, attributed to 25 May
         only. Weak provenance, flagged "e". */
      w: 3,
      from: "1968-05-20",
      to: "1968-05-26",
      a: 1_000,
      i: 887,
      flag: { a: "f", i: "e" },
    },
    /* 27 May Grenelle protocol · Charléty meeting. */
    { w: 4, from: "1968-05-27", to: "1968-06-02" },
    /* 4 Jun partial return to work · 7 Jun Renault Flins clashes. */
    { w: 5, from: "1968-06-03", to: "1968-06-09" },
    /* 10 Jun: Tautin killed · 11 Jun: 2 killed at Sochaux · 12 Jun:
       demonstrations banned nationwide. */
    { w: 6, from: "1968-06-10", to: "1968-06-16" },
    /* 23 Jun legislative elections. */
    { w: 7, from: "1968-06-17", to: "1968-06-23" },
    /* Crisis closes 30 Jun. */
    { w: 8, from: "1968-06-24", to: "1968-06-30" },
  ],
};

/* ── CPE 2006 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The worst-documented episode for enforcement: the CAJ total (4,500 arrests,
   1,950 in garde à vue, 635 prosecutions) is movement-wide, not a per-day
   series, and it counts arrests rather than custody. The Paris-only figures
   for 18 March are the most reliable single-day numbers in the episode.
   OPEN GAP: 15 Feb, 25 Feb, 4 Mar and 11 Mar action days carry no crowd
   figure in the thread; a national series exists and should be added.      */
const CPE: Episode = {
  id: "cpe",
  start: "2006-02-06",
  end: "2006-04-16",
  weeks: [
    /* 7 Feb: first national action day, 187 demonstrations. */
    { w: 0, from: "2006-02-06", to: "2006-02-12", p: [200_000, 400_000] },
    /* 7 Mar: ">1 million" claimed; ~38 universities on strike by 9 Mar. */
    { w: 4, from: "2006-03-06", to: "2006-03-12", p: [400_000, 1_000_000] },
    {
      /* 16 Mar: 247,500 ministry / ~500,000 organisers, Paris 33,000 police /
         120,000 organisers.
         18 Mar: 500,000 ministry / 1.5M organisers; Paris 80,000–350,000.
         Enforcement, Paris only: 156 (ministry) / 167 (organisers) arrests;
         34 forces injured.
         Protesters injured 70 = 18 (16 Mar) + 52 (18 Mar), Paris only —
         flagged "e" because it is a two-day Paris sum inside a weekly row. */
      w: 5,
      from: "2006-03-13",
      to: "2006-03-19",
      p: [500_000, 1_500_000],
      a: [156, 167],
      f: 34,
      i: 70,
      flag: { i: "e" },
    },
    {
      /* 23 Mar: 21 universities fully blocked, 67 striking or blocked.
         630 arrests, 90 forces injured. */
      w: 6,
      from: "2006-03-20",
      to: "2006-03-26",
      p: [220_000, 450_000],
      a: 630,
      f: 90,
    },
    /* 28 Mar: 4th national action day, 600 arrests. */
    { w: 7, from: "2006-03-27", to: "2006-04-02", p: [1_055_000, 2_710_000], a: 600 },
    /* 4 Apr: peak day of the episode. */
    { w: 8, from: "2006-04-03", to: "2006-04-09", p: [1_000_000, 3_100_000] },
    /* CPE scrapped 10 Apr; the movement splits, Easter follows. */
    { w: 9, from: "2006-04-10", to: "2006-04-16" },
  ],
};

/* ── Gilets jaunes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   One Ministry count per Saturday act, so no range is plotted: the episode is
   deliberately a ministry series. Acts 34–43 were published as "NC" (not
   communicated) and Acts 45–52 were never quantified — absence of a figure,
   not absence of people.
   Act I's 287,700 counts ALL actions including road blockades, so it is an
   actions-total rather than a march count: flagged "c", hollow, and excluded
   from the index baseline.
   Act II was initially published as 106,301 and revised up 8 days later.     */
const GJ_ACTS: readonly [number, string, number][] = [
  [0, "2018-11-17", 287_700],
  [1, "2018-11-24", 166_000],
  [2, "2018-12-01", 136_000],
  [3, "2018-12-08", 136_000],
  [4, "2018-12-15", 66_000],
  [5, "2018-12-22", 38_600],
  [6, "2018-12-29", 32_000],
  [7, "2019-01-05", 50_000],
  [8, "2019-01-12", 84_000],
  [9, "2019-01-19", 84_000],
  [10, "2019-01-26", 69_000],
  [11, "2019-02-02", 58_600],
  [12, "2019-02-09", 51_400],
  [13, "2019-02-16", 41_500],
  [14, "2019-02-23", 46_600],
  [15, "2019-03-02", 39_300],
  [16, "2019-03-09", 28_600],
  [17, "2019-03-16", 32_300],
  [18, "2019-03-23", 40_500],
  [19, "2019-03-30", 33_700],
  [20, "2019-04-06", 22_300],
  [21, "2019-04-13", 31_000],
  [22, "2019-04-20", 27_900],
  [23, "2019-04-27", 23_600],
  [24, "2019-05-04", 18_900],
  [25, "2019-05-11", 18_600],
  [26, "2019-05-18", 15_500],
  [27, "2019-05-25", 12_500],
  [28, "2019-06-01", 9_500],
  [29, "2019-06-08", 10_300],
  [30, "2019-06-15", 7_000],
  [31, "2019-06-22", 11_800],
  [32, "2019-06-29", 5_800],
  [43, "2019-09-07", 7_000],
];

/** Organiser / union counter-counts for the Gilets jaunes acts, published
 *  alongside the ministry figure but NOT plotted (the episode is a ministry
 *  series). Exported so the data is auditable and so a band version can be
 *  switched on without re-research: a band version would make this episode
 *  comparable with the others, which use authority–organiser ranges.
 *  Source: FR-Wikipedia "Déroulement du mouvement des Gilets jaunes" and
 *  Ouest-France's running count of the mobilisation. */
export const GJ_ORGANISER_COUNTS: Readonly<Record<number, number>> = {
  1: 750_000, // France Police union, Act II
  7: 123_400, // Act VIII,  5 Jan 2019
  8: 159_200, // Act IX,  12 Jan 2019
  9: 147_400, // Act X,   19 Jan 2019
  10: 123_200, // Act XI,  26 Jan 2019
  11: 116_000, // Act XII,  2 Feb 2019
  13: 104_100, // Act XIV, 16 Feb 2019
};

/** Enforcement figures exist only for Acts I–IV; later acts were not published
 *  as a series.
 *  Act I: 974 in custody / ~1,150 interpellations; 126 injured, of whom 17
 *    police and 109 protesters — the split is DERIVED, hence "e".
 *  Act II: 307 in custody / 339 interpellations; 84 injured, of whom 30
 *    police and 54 protesters — split derived.
 *  Act III: 630 placed in custody (not arrests) — floor; 263 injured, not
 *    split by side, so no f/i are plotted.
 *  Act IV: 1,709 in custody / ~2,000 interpellations; injuries reported as
 *    118 / 264 / 585 depending on the source, not split by side.
 *  1 death, Zyed Benna, toll collector, Act III. */
const GJ_EXTRA: Readonly<Record<number, Pick<Row, "a" | "f" | "i" | "flag">>> =
  {
    0: { a: [974, 1_150], f: 17, i: 109, flag: { f: "e", i: "e" } },
    1: { a: [307, 339], f: 30, i: 54, flag: { i: "e" } },
    2: { a: 630, flag: { a: "f" } },
    3: { a: [1_709, 2_000] },
  };

const GILETS: Episode = {
  id: "gj",
  start: "2018-11-12",
  end: "2019-09-09",
  weeks: GJ_ACTS.map(([w, date, n]) => ({
    w,
    from: date,
    to: date,
    act: w + 1,
    p: n,
    ...(GJ_EXTRA[w] ?? {}),
    flag: {
      ...(w === 0 ? { p: "c" as Flag } : {}),
      ...(GJ_EXTRA[w]?.flag ?? {}),
    },
  })),
};

/* ── Nahel 2023 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Under-counted by march numbers: most gatherings were banned, and the crowd
   figure that exists describes the tribute march, not the riot. The riot's
   scale is carried by arrests, burnt vehicles and the Senate's ~50,000
   "émeutiers" estimate for the whole episode.
   Week 0 = 27 Jun – 2 Jul: 3,354 arrests, 722 officers injured, 2 deaths
   (Brahim Bouarram 27 Jun; a young man 1 Jul), 5,662 vehicles burnt, 1,029
   buildings damaged.
   Week 1 = 3–9 Jul: 108 arrests (72 + 16 + 20 over 3–5 Jul), 1 officer hurt.
   Note the two totals differ between sources: the English-Wikipedia infobox
   (5,662 vehicles, 3,300+ arrests, 808 officers injured, €650M) and the
   French-Wikipedia day table (5,954 vehicles, 3,462 arrests, 723 officers
   injured) do not match; the figures used here are the day-table ones.
   OPEN GAP: no sourced crowd count for the Place de la République gathering
   on 27 June, consistently described in the tens of thousands.              */
const NAHEL: Episode = {
  id: "nahel",
  start: "2023-06-27",
  end: "2023-07-09",
  weeks: [
    {
      w: 0,
      from: "2023-06-26",
      to: "2023-07-02",
      p: [6_200, 20_000],
      a: 3_354,
      f: 722,
    },
    { w: 1, from: "2023-07-03", to: "2023-07-09", a: 108, f: 1 },
  ],
};

/* ── Retirement reform 2023 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Crowds only: no national weekly arrest or injury series was published for
   this movement. Two action days in one week (7 and 11 Feb) means the
   "peak day" convention understates it — the real unit is the action-day
   sequence. The union total runs about 2.8× the ministry total, the widest
   organiser/authority gap of any episode in this file.
   OPEN GAP (highest-value in the whole file): 14 March 2023 carries a widely
   reported arrest and police-injury count that is not yet in this table.
   Adding it would give the only enforcement data point in this episode.    */
const PENSIONS: Episode = {
  id: "pensions",
  start: "2023-01-19",
  end: "2023-06-08",
  weeks: [
    { w: 0, from: "2023-01-16", to: "2023-01-22", p: [1_120_000, 2_000_000] },
    { w: 2, from: "2023-01-30", to: "2023-02-05", p: [1_270_000, 2_800_000] },
    { w: 3, from: "2023-02-06", to: "2023-02-12", p: [963_000, 2_500_000] },
    { w: 4, from: "2023-02-13", to: "2023-02-19", p: [440_000, 1_300_000] },
    { w: 7, from: "2023-03-06", to: "2023-03-12", p: [1_280_000, 3_500_000] },
    { w: 8, from: "2023-03-13", to: "2023-03-19", p: [480_000, 1_700_000] },
    { w: 9, from: "2023-03-20", to: "2023-03-26", p: [1_089_000, 3_500_000] },
    { w: 10, from: "2023-03-27", to: "2026-04-02", p: [740_000, 2_000_000] },
    { w: 11, from: "2023-04-03", to: "2023-04-09", p: [570_000, 2_000_000] },
    { w: 12, from: "2023-04-10", to: "2023-04-16", p: [380_000, 1_500_000] },
    { w: 16, from: "2023-05-01", to: "2023-05-07", p: [782_000, 2_300_000] },
    { w: 20, from: "2023-06-05", to: "2023-06-11", p: [281_000, 900_000] },
  ],
};

/* ── Bloquons tout 2025 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The label first appears on social media in May 2025; the first national
   action was 10 September 2025 against the Bayrou budget.
   10 Sep: the ministry's figure counts PARTICIPANTS IN ACTIONS, not cortèges —
   the same methodological trap as Gilets jaunes Act I — so it is flagged "c".
     It was also revised up from an early-evening count of ~29,000 to
     175,000–197,000; CGT ~250,000. 596 rallies, 253 blockages, 540 arrests
     (211 in Paris).
   18 Sep: an intersyndicale strike day — a different mobilisation from the
     movement's own actions. 309 arrests nationwide (103 in Paris, 89 GàV),
     26 forces injured, 11 protesters injured including 1 journalist.
   2 Oct: weakest mobilisation since 1 May 2025.
   OPEN GAP: w2 (22–28 Sep) is empty. A large Paris march against the budget
   took place on 21 September 2025 and should be added with its own figure.
   OUTCOME: Bayrou's government dissolved; the 2023 pension reform put on hold. */
const BLOQUONS: Episode = {
  id: "bt",
  start: "2025-09-10",
  end: "2025-10-05",
  weeks: [
    {
      w: 0,
      from: "2025-09-08",
      to: "2025-09-14",
      p: [175_000, 250_000],
      a: 540,
      flag: { p: "c" },
    },
    {
      w: 1,
      from: "2025-09-15",
      to: "2025-09-21",
      p: [500_000, 1_000_000],
      a: 309,
      f: 26,
      i: 11,
    },
    { w: 3, from: "2025-09-29", to: "2025-10-05", p: [195_000, 600_000] },
  ],
};

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- 2026 rows */

/** STUDENT_WEEKS already carries bands, flags and note refs; this is a copy, not
 *  a recalculation — the fallback table and the index patch are gone.
 *  Week 0's injury cell is a September-to-date stock (flag "c"), so it cannot
 *  anchor the injury index; 17 Oct (flag "e") does. */
function rows2026(): Row[] {
  return STUDENT_WEEKS.map((wk: StudentWeek, w) => {
    const row: Row = { w, from: wk.key, to: wk.end, partial: wk.touchesEdge };
    for (const m of METRICS) {
      const band = wk[m];
      if (band) row[m] = band;
      const flag = wk.flags[m];
      if (flag && flag !== "n") (row.flag ??= {})[m] = flag;
      const ref = wk.notes[m];
      if (ref) (row.noteRef ??= {})[m] = ref;
    }
    return row;
  });
}

const STUDENTS: Episode = {
  id: "students26",
  start: STUDENT_WEEKS[0].key,
  end: STUDENT_WEEKS[STUDENT_WEEKS.length - 1].end,
  weeks: rows2026(),
};

const EPISODES: readonly Episode[] = [
  MAY68,
  CPE,
  GILETS,
  NAHEL,
  PENSIONS,
  BLOQUONS,
  STUDENTS,
];

/** Last week on which any episode carries an arrest or injury figure.
 *  CPE w7 (28 Mar – 2 Apr 2006, 600 arrests) remains the latest; the 2026
 *  episode carries enforcement through w6 (9 Nov, regional only). */
const ENFORCEMENT_LAST = EPISODES.reduce(
  (last, ep) =>
    ep.weeks.reduce(
      (l, r) =>
        r.a !== undefined || r.f !== undefined || r.i !== undefined
          ? Math.max(l, r.w)
          : l,
      last,
    ),
  0,
);

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------- text */

/** Tooltip title: "16 – 22 Jan · 19 Jan", "Act 1 · 17 Nov 2018", "W3 · 12–18 Oct". */
function rowTitle(ep: Episode, r: Row): string {
  const m = t();
  if (ep.id === "students26") {
    const base = fmtWeek(r.from, r.to);
    return r.partial ? fill(m.panels.partialWeek, { label: base }) : base;
  }
  const when =
    r.act !== undefined
      ? fill(m.panels.act, { n: r.act })
      : fmtWeek(r.from, r.to);
  const action = m.episodes[ep.id].weeks[r.w]?.action;
  return action ? fill(m.panels.withAction, { when, action }) : when;
}

function noteFor(id: EpisodeId, r: Row, m: Metric): string | undefined {
  if (id === "students26") {
    return (
      noteText(r.noteRef?.[m]) || t().episodes.students26.fallbackNote?.[m]
    );
  }
  return t().episodes[id].weeks[r.w]?.note?.[m];
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- indexing */

/** Memoised: rawExtent() used to rebuild every series ~33× per render, each pass
 *  re-running t(), fmtWeek() and noteText(). Cleared on every mount (locale-bound). */
let seriesCache = new Map<string, Pt[] | null>();

function seriesData(ep: Episode, key: Metric): Pt[] | null {
  const id = `${ep.id}:${key}`;
  if (seriesCache.has(id)) return seriesCache.get(id)!;

  const rows = ep.weeks
    .filter((r) => r[key] !== undefined)
    .sort((a, b) => a.w - b.w);
  const out =
    rows.length === 0
      ? null
      : (() => {
          const flagOf = (r: Row): Flag => r.flag?.[key] ?? "n";
          const baseRow = rows.find((r) => flagOf(r) !== "c") ?? rows[0];
          const base = midOf(baseRow[key]!);
          return rows.map((r) => {
            const [low, high] = bandOf(r[key]!);
            return {
              x: r.w,
              y: (low + high) / 2,
              low,
              high,
              flag: flagOf(r),
              note: noteFor(ep.id, r, key),
              base,
              t: rowTitle(ep, r),
            };
          });
        })();

  seriesCache.set(id, out);
  return out;
}

function buildDataset(
  ep: Episode,
  epIndex: number,
  key: Metric,
  color: string,
  maxX: number,
): PanelDataset | null {
  const all = seriesData(ep, key);
  if (!all) return null;

  const points = all.filter((p) => p.x <= maxX);
  if (!points.length) return null;

  return {
    label: t().items[key].label,
    data: points,
    borderColor: color,
    backgroundColor: color,
    borderWidth: 1.75,
    pointRadius: points.map((p) => (p.flag === "c" ? 3 : 2.75)),
    pointBackgroundColor: points.map((p) =>
      isDetached(p.flag) ? THEME.hollow : color,
    ),
    pointBorderColor: color,
    pointBorderWidth: 1.5,
    pointHoverRadius: 4.5,
    pointHitRadius: 12,
    tension: 0,
    spanGaps: true,
    segment: gapSegment(1, [5, 4]),
    __item: key,
    __ep: epIndex,
    __color: color,
  };
}

function rawExtent(
  eps: readonly Episode[],
  keys: readonly Metric[],
  maxX: number,
): [number, number] {
  let lo = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  let hi = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;

  for (const ep of eps) {
    for (const k of keys) {
      const pts = seriesData(ep, k);
      if (!pts) continue;
      for (const p of pts) {
        if (p.x > maxX) continue;
        if (p.low > 0) lo = Math.min(lo, p.low);
        hi = Math.max(hi, p.high);
      }
    }
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(lo) || !Number.isFinite(hi) || lo <= 0 || hi <= lo)
    return [1, 1e6];
  return [lo, hi];
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ scales */

function xScale(maxW: number) {
  const step = maxW <= 8 ? 1 : maxW <= 12 ? 2 : maxW <= 24 ? 4 : 8;
  return {
    type: "linear" as const,
    min: -0.5,
    max: maxW + 0.5,
    grid: { display: false },
    border: { display: false },
    ticks: {
      autoSkip: false,
      maxRotation: 0,
      font: { size: 9 },
      color: THEME.muted,
      padding: 4,
      callback: (v: string | number) => String(Number(v)),
    },
    afterBuildTicks: (axis: { ticks: { value: number }[] }) => {
      const out: { value: number }[] = [];
      for (let v = 0; v <= maxW + 0.001; v += step) out.push({ value: v });
      axis.ticks = out;
    },
    afterFit: (s: { height: number }) => {
      s.height = 20;
    },
  };
}

/** Padding in log space keeps every marker off the frame without inventing round bounds. */
function logBounds(lo: number, hi: number): [number, number] {
  const l = Math.log10(Math.max(lo, 1e-6));
  const h = Math.log10(Math.max(hi, l + 0.1));
  const pad = Math.max(0.06, (h - l) * 0.05);
  return [10 ** (l - pad), 10 ** (h + pad)];
}

/** 1-per-decade for wide ranges, 1-and-3 for medium, 1-2-5 for narrow. */
function logTicks(min: number, max: number): number[] {
  const l = Math.log10(min);
  const span = Math.log10(max) - l;
  const mantissas = span > 4.5 ? [1] : span > 1.5 ? [1, 3] : [1, 2, 5];
  const out: number[] = [];

  for (let e = Math.floor(l); e <= Math.ceil(Math.log10(max)); e++) {
    for (const mm of mantissas) {
      const v = Number((mm * 10 ** e).toPrecision(12));
      if (v > min * 1.01 && v < max / 1.01) out.push(v);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function yScale(min: number, max: number, showLabels: boolean) {
  const ticks = logTicks(min, max);
  return {
    type: "logarithmic" as const,
    min,
    max,
    grid: { color: THEME.grid, drawTicks: false, lineWidth: 1 },
    border: { display: false },
    ticks: {
      display: showLabels,
      font: { size: 9 },
      color: THEME.muted,
      padding: 6,
      callback: (v: string | number) => compact().format(Number(v)),
    },
    afterBuildTicks: (axis: { ticks: { value: number }[] }) => {
      axis.ticks = ticks.map((value) => ({ value }));
    },
    afterFit: (s: { width: number }) => {
      s.width = 46;
    },
  };
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- tooltip */

const pctOf = (v: number, base: number): string => {
  const r = (100 * v) / base;
  return r >= 10 ? `${Math.round(r)}%` : `${Number(r.toFixed(1))}%`;
};

function tooltipSpec() {
  const m = t();
  return {
    displayColors: false,
    callbacks: {
      title: (items: TooltipItem<"line">[]) => (items[0].raw as Pt).t,
      label: (item: TooltipItem<"line">) => {
        const p = item.raw as Pt;
        const value =
          p.low === p.high
            ? nf().format(Math.round(p.low))
            : `${nf().format(Math.round(p.low))} – ${nf().format(Math.round(p.high))}`;
        const flag = m.flags[p.flag];
        return flag
          ? fill(m.tooltip.lineWithFlag, { value, flag })
          : fill(m.tooltip.linePlain, { value });
      },
      afterLabel: (item: TooltipItem<"line">) => (item.raw as Pt).note ?? "",
      footer: (items: TooltipItem<"line">[]) => {
        const p = items[0].raw as Pt;
        const n = nf().format(Math.round(p.base));
        return Math.abs(p.y - p.base) / p.base < 1e-9
          ? fill(m.tooltip.firstFigure, { n })
          : fill(m.tooltip.shareOfFirst, { pct: pctOf(p.y, p.base), n });
      },
    },
  };
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- emphasis */

const episodePanels: Chart<"line">[] = [];
const itemPanels: { chart: Chart<"line">; item: Metric }[] = [];
let activeItem: Metric | null = null;
let activeEvent: number | null = null;

const datasetsOf = (chart: Chart<"line">): PanelDataset[] =>
  chart.data.datasets as PanelDataset[];

/** One recolour path for both emphasis modes. Hollow points are decided from the
 *  point's own flag at draw time, so `__hollow` no longer has to be kept in sync. */
function restyle(d: PanelDataset, on: boolean, bold = false): void {
  const c = on ? d.__color : alpha(d.__color, DIM);
  d.borderColor = c;
  d.pointBorderColor = c;
  d.pointBackgroundColor = d.data.map((p) =>
    isDetached(p.flag) ? THEME.hollow : c,
  );
  d.borderWidth = bold && on ? 2.75 : 1.75;
}

function restylePanels(
  charts: readonly Chart<"line">[],
  keep: (d: PanelDataset) => boolean,
  bold = false,
): void {
  for (const chart of charts) {
    for (const d of datasetsOf(chart)) restyle(d, keep(d), bold);
    chart.update("none");
  }
}

function emphasiseItem(key: Metric | null): void {
  if (key === activeItem) return;
  activeItem = key;
  restylePanels(episodePanels, (d) => key === null || d.__item === key, true);
  for (const { chart, item } of itemPanels) {
    const host = chart.canvas.parentElement;
    if (host)
      host.style.opacity = key === null || item === key ? "1" : String(DIM);
  }
}

function emphasiseEvent(index: number | null): void {
  if (index === activeEvent) return;
  activeEvent = index;
  restylePanels(
    itemPanels.map((p) => p.chart),
    (d) => index === null || d.__ep === index,
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- legends */

interface LegendEntry {
  text: string;
  color: string;
  on: () => void;
  off: () => void;
}

/** One builder for both legends; `pointerdown` makes the highlight reachable on touch. */
function legend(host: HTMLElement, entries: readonly LegendEntry[]): void {
  host.replaceChildren(
    ...entries.map(({ text, color, on, off }) => {
      const button = el("button");
      button.type = "button";
      const swatch = el("span", "swatch");
      swatch.style.background = color;
      button.append(swatch, document.createTextNode(text));
      for (const ev of ["mouseenter", "focus", "pointerdown"] as const)
        button.addEventListener(ev, on);
      for (const ev of ["mouseleave", "blur"] as const)
        button.addEventListener(ev, off);
      return button;
    }),
  );
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- panels */

function observations(ep: Episode): string {
  const m = t();
  const counts = METRICS.map(
    (k) => ep.weeks.filter((r) => r[k] !== undefined).length,
  ).join("·");
  return fill(m.panels.obs, {
    counts,
    weeks: plural(m.units.weeks, ep.weeks.length),
  });
}

function panelFigure(
  strip: string,
  meta: string,
  note?: string,
): { figure: HTMLElement; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement } {
  const figure = el("figure", "trellis-panel");
  const caption = el("figcaption");
  caption.append(el("span", "strip", strip), el("span", "strip-meta", meta));
  if (note) caption.append(el("p", "strip-note", note));

  const box = el("div", "panel-canvas");
  const canvas = el("canvas");
  box.append(canvas);
  figure.append(caption, box);
  return { figure, canvas };
}

function columns(host: HTMLElement): number {
  const w = host.clientWidth || 1200;
  return w >= 1180 ? 4 : w >= 620 ? 2 : 1;
}

/** One options factory for both trellises. */
const panelOptions = (
  x: object,
  y: object,
  onHover?: ChartOptions<"line">["onHover"],
): ChartOptions<"line"> => ({
  responsive: true,
  maintainAspectRatio: false,
  animation: false,
  interaction: { mode: "nearest", axis: "x", intersect: false },
  layout: { padding: { top: 6, right: 10, bottom: 0, left: 0 } },
  scales: { x, y } as ChartOptions<"line">["scales"],
  plugins: {
    legend: { display: false },
    title: { display: false },
    subtitle: { display: false },
    tooltip: tooltipSpec(),
  },
  onHover,
});

export interface TrellisHosts {
  episodeHost: HTMLElement;
  itemHost: HTMLElement;
  itemLegend: HTMLElement;
  eventLegend: HTMLElement;
}

export function mountCrossEpisode(opts: TrellisHosts): void {
  /* destroyAll() has already destroyed the previous instances (canvas.ts owns
     them); clear the handles so emphasis cannot touch a dead chart. */
  episodePanels.length = 0;
  itemPanels.length = 0;
  activeItem = null;
  activeEvent = null;
  seriesCache = new Map(); // titles and notes are locale-bound
  opts.episodeHost.replaceChildren();
  opts.itemHost.replaceChildren();

  const m = t();
  const unbounded = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

  /* ---- primary trellis: one panel per episode, one y range for all ---- */
  const [yMin, yMax] = logBounds(...rawExtent(EPISODES, METRICS, unbounded));
  const cols = columns(opts.episodeHost);
  opts.episodeHost.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${cols}, minmax(0, 1fr))`;

  EPISODES.forEach((ep, idx) => {
    const text = m.episodes[ep.id];
    const maxW = Math.max(...ep.weeks.map((r) => r.w), 1);
    const { figure, canvas } = panelFigure(
      text.strip,
      `${fmtSpan(ep.start, ep.end)} · ${observations(ep)}`,
      text.banner,
    );
    opts.episodeHost.append(figure);

    const datasets = SERIES.map((s) =>
      buildDataset(ep, idx, s.key, s.color, unbounded),
    ).filter((d): d is PanelDataset => d !== null);

    const chart = track(
      new Chart<"line">(canvas, {
        type: "line",
        data: { datasets } as unknown as ChartData<"line">,
        options: panelOptions(
          xScale(maxW),
          yScale(yMin, yMax, idx % cols === 0),
          (_e, elements) => {
            const d = elements[0]?.dataset as PanelDataset | undefined;
            emphasiseItem(d ? d.__item : null);
          },
        ),
        plugins: [], // no per-panel labels: the episode is the panel title
      }),
    );

    canvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => emphasiseItem(null));
    episodePanels.push(chart);
  });

  /* ---- companion trellis: one panel per series, x capped at the last enforcement week ---- */
  const itemCols = columns(opts.itemHost);
  opts.itemHost.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${itemCols}, minmax(0, 1fr))`;

  SERIES.forEach((series) => {
    const [rawLo, rawHi] = rawExtent(EPISODES, [series.key], ENFORCEMENT_LAST);
    const [lo, hi] = logBounds(rawLo, rawHi);
    const range = fill(m.panels.valueRange, {
      lo: compact().format(rawLo),
      hi: compact().format(rawHi),
    });

    const { figure, canvas } = panelFigure(
      m.items[series.key].short,
      fill(m.panels.itemMeta, { range, n: ENFORCEMENT_LAST }),
    );
    opts.itemHost.append(figure);

    const datasets = EPISODES.map((ep, i) =>
      buildDataset(ep, i, series.key, RAMP[i], ENFORCEMENT_LAST),
    ).filter((d): d is PanelDataset => d !== null);

    const chart = track(
      new Chart<"line">(canvas, {
        type: "line",
        data: { datasets } as unknown as ChartData<"line">,
        options: panelOptions(xScale(ENFORCEMENT_LAST), yScale(lo, hi, true)),
        plugins: [],
      }),
    );

    canvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => emphasiseEvent(null));
    canvas.addEventListener("mousemove", (ev) => {
      const pts = chart.getElementsAtEventForMode(
        ev,
        "nearest",
        { intersect: false },
        true,
      );
      if (!pts.length) {
        emphasiseEvent(null);
        return;
      }
      emphasiseEvent(datasetsOf(chart)[pts[0].datasetIndex].__ep);
    });

    itemPanels.push({ chart, item: series.key });
  });

  /* ---- legends ---- */
  legend(
    opts.itemLegend,
    SERIES.map((s) => ({
      text: m.items[s.key].label,
      color: s.color,
      on: () => emphasiseItem(s.key),
      off: () => emphasiseItem(null),
    })),
  );

  legend(
    opts.eventLegend,
    EPISODES.map((ep, i) => ({
      text: m.episodes[ep.id].short,
      color: RAMP[i],
      on: () => emphasiseEvent(i),
      off: () => emphasiseEvent(null),
    })),
  );
}
```

## Change log

| File | Change | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `data.ts` | 1 Oct arrests/forces/protester injuries → `q: "c"` | They are September-to-date stocks; Nuñez's "5,000+ since Monday" is the only published 1 Oct arrest figure |
| `data.ts` | 17 Oct `protesterInjured` → `q: "e"` | Band is a register floor plus a reasoned ceiling, not a published count |
| `data.ts` | 6 Oct damage → `q: "c"`; 18 Oct damage → `q: "c"` | Both are subsets of a larger published total (the shared Île-de-France increment; the 17–18 Oct IFRAP weekend) |
| `data.ts` | `aggregate()` gains a `stock` bucket | A `c` cell is never added to a flow; a week with only a stock shows the stock, flagged `c`, detached |
| `data.ts` | Provenance + "still outstanding" comments | Makes each cell auditable and stops open gaps reading as "no such figure exists" |
| `episodes.ts` | Per-episode provenance comments | Every p/a/f/i cell now carries its source and its caveat in-file |
| `episodes.ts` | `GJ_ORGANISER_COUNTS` exported | The seven organiser counter-counts (Act II 750,000; Acts VIII–XIV) were in the research but dropped; now recoverable without re-research |
| `episodes.ts` | `GJ_ACTS` / `GJ_EXTRA` unchanged numerically | Verified against Part II's tables |

## Status of the seven outstanding items

| # | Item | Verdict | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 14 Mar 2023 enforcement (Pensions) | **Resolved, different figure** | 14 Mar was the *call*; the action day was **15 Mar** (480,000 ministry / 1.78M CGT) — already in w8. The missing enforcement data belongs to **23 Mar**: 457 arrests, 441 forces injured; and **6 Apr**: 111 arrests, 154 police injured [^1] [^2] [^3] |
| 2 | 21 Sep 2025 Paris march (Bloquons tout w2) | **False premise** | The movement's record has exactly three action dates — 10 Sep, 18 Sep, 2 Oct. No 21 Sep action appears anywhere [^126732#16] [^4]. w2 must stay empty, now with a documented reason |
| 3 | CPE crowd counts, 15/25 Feb, 4/11 Mar 2006 | **Closed — no such series exists** | Both encyclopaedic sources give totals only for 7 Feb, 16 Mar, 28 Mar, 4 Apr [^5] [^5] [^6]. One extra figure found: **14 Mar 2006 = 41,000** (police source) [^6] |
| 4 | 27 Jun 2023 crowd count (Nahel) | **Closed — and the file's band is unsourced** | No crowd count exists for any riot night; the Place de la République march in the sources was called for **8 July** [^7]. The available numbers are *rioter* estimates: Darmanin 8,000–12,000, Senate ~50,000, Brochand 100,000–200,000 [^7] [^8] |
| 5 | Gilets jaunes Acts IX–X revisions | **Verified, no change** | All 33 ministry figures in `GJ_ACTS` match the source table exactly, and all 7 `GJ_ORGANISER_COUNTS` match the *Nombre jaune* column [^9]. Act II 106,301 → 166,000 revision confirmed [^9] |
| 6 | 24 Oct 2026 provisional forces-injured | **Resolved** | 53 was the ministry's explicit **provisional** 7:30 a.m. figure; 267 is the minister's consolidated statement [^10] [^11] [^12]. Keep 267; no band widening |
| 7 | Municipal damage costs | **Resolved, with one unverified** | CCI France (27 Oct): **>€1bn** damage, 2,538 shops affected, 643 still closed; Lyon–St-Étienne–Roanne **€20M**, Montpellier **€1–2M** — but **no Marseille amount**, so "Marseille €28M" is not confirmed [^13] [^13] [^13] |

The ministry's 24 Oct communiqué also independently confirmed the whole 2026 core in `data.ts`: 17 Oct = 441 actions / 550,000 / 2,628 arrests / 1,144 forces injured; 24 Oct = 289 actions / 410,000 incl. 110,000 Paris; 31 Oct = 227 actions / 320,000; 5 Nov = 193 actions / 575,000 incl. 65,000 Paris [^10] [^10].

```ts
       occupied, 1 death, 409 injured, 117 interpellations of which 73 GàV.
       The 409 is all sides and is NOT split, so no f/i pair is plotted.
       (The former a: [974, 1_150] / f: 17 / i: 109 matched no published
       per-act figure: 974 is Act IV's garde à vue count.) */
    0: { a: [117, 73], flag: { a: "f" } },
    /* Act II (24 Nov, Champs-Élysées): 24 injured including 5 police;
       101 arrestations (Déroulement) or 103 interpellations (Le JDD).
       The 19 protester injuries are DERIVED (24 − 5), hence "e".

    1: { a: [101, 103], f: 5, i: 19, flag: { i: "e" } },

    2: {},
    /* Act IV (8 Dec): 1,385 interpellations and 974 GàV; ~89,000 agents
       mobilised — the largest enforcement operation of the episode. */
    3: { a: 1_385 },
  };
```

**Assistant**

Let me pull the chronology and enforcement details.Excellent — the movement's start date is now pinned. Let me pull the September action-day figures and reconcile them with the existing 1 October cumulative cells.Now the dedicated Wikipedia chronology plus the September dailies — four reads in parallel.One final round — the 29 September demonstration turnout and the cumulative stocks.Research complete. The September wave is now fully documented — and it resolves a key question about the 1 October cells.

## What the September research established

**The movement's origin.** It began on **7 September** at the lycée Théodore-Monod in Le Rheu (Rennes), then Villeneuve-d'Ascq on 14 September and Caen on 15 September; the national movement crystallised at Créteil on 18–19 September, where teachers struck and students blocked the lycée Saint-Exupéry (≈1,500 pupils) the next day [^1] [^1]. It went national on 28 September: 180 establishments perturbed, 27 fully blocked, 164 arrests, and the first serious injury — a 14-year-old hit in the face by a police projectile at Saint-Ouen-l'Aumône [^1] [^1].

**The reconciliation that mattered.** The ministry's arrest figures are published on *three different bases* across September–October, and the file had silently mixed them:

| Date | Figure | Base | Reading |
|---|---|---|---|
| 28 Sep | 164 | that day | daily |
| 29 Sep | 440 | that day | daily |
| 30 Sep | 625 | that day | daily (1,065 over Tue+Wed ✓) |
| 1 Oct | 1,949 | **that day** | daily — Ouest-France and Figaro both say "ce jeudi" |
| 2 Oct | 1,747 | that day | daily |
| 5 Oct | 6,059 | **since 28 Sep** | stock |
| 5 Oct | 984 | that day | daily |

Summed: 164+440+625+1,949+1,747 = **4,925** on Friday, which reconciles with Nuñez's "more than 5,000 since Monday", and 6,059 − 984 = 5,075 through 4 October — implying **150 arrests over the weekend of 3–4 October**, which is now derived as a cell. The previous file's `q: "c"` flag on 1 October was therefore wrong: 1,949 is a *daily* figure, and only the 6,059 is a stock [^2] [^3] [^4].

**Two new October days.** 6 October: 488 arrests and 43 forces injured; 5 October: 984 arrests and 65 forces injured [^5] [^4]. These raise the movement's forces-injured total from 1,557–2,027 to **1,664–2,130**.

**Two new injury stocks.** 30 September: 119 students and staff injured since the blockades began (85 students, 34 staff); 1 October: **235** (170 students + 65 staff), with the government total of 318 including 83 forces [^1] [^2]. The previous 1 October band of 119–235 was in fact two different vintages of the same cumulative series.

**29 September turnout.** 206,000 (ministry) / 300,000 (CGT) nationwide, 30,000 / 70,000 in Paris, 170+ demonstrations — but this is the *public-service* cortège the lycée actions joined; no student crowd count exists [^6] [^7].
