## `data.ts`

```ts
/* Every figure and every projection. Sections A/B (chart.ts) and C (episodes.ts)
   both read these, which is why section C no longer needs a fallback table, an
   index patch applied to derived values, or its own recalculation of flags.

   ── Verification pass, 12 Nov 2026 ─────────────────────────────────────────
   Every cell below is carried from the research threads "French Protest Charts"
   Parts I and II, where each figure is cited to the Interior Ministry day-by-day
   bilans, IFRAP, prefecture bulletins or the press. Three corrections were
   applied in the first pass; each is marked FIX at its cell.

   ── Second pass, 12 Nov 2026: the window now opens on 15 September 2026 ───
   The 2026 lycée/student movement is traced back to its origins: the first
   mainland blockades were Le Rheu (7 Sep), Villeneuve-d'Ascq (14 Sep) and Caen
   (15 Sep); the national movement crystallised at Créteil on 18–19 Sep and
   went national on 28 Sep. Four corrections follow from that:

   FIX 5: 1 Oct arrests are a DAILY figure (1,949, "ce jeudi"), not a stock.
          The only published stock is 6,059 "since 28 September" (5 Oct). The
          daily series now runs 164 / 440 / 625 / 1,949 / 1,747 / [weekend
          150, derived] / 984 / 488 and sums to 6,547 through 6 Oct, which
          reproduces the ministry's own 6,059 + 488 exactly.
   FIX 6: the 1 Oct injury figures were two vintages of ONE cumulative series:
          119 (30 Sep: 85 students + 34 staff) and 235 (1 Oct: 170 students +
          65 staff). They are now separate stock cells on their own dates.
   FIX 7: the 1 Oct forces-injured band 84–305 mixed a Wednesday cumulative
          level (83) with a Thursday daily flow (~300). Kept as one band,
          re-flagged "e", and the 83 is no longer added a second time.
   FIX 8: 5 and 6 Oct carry their own daily enforcement figures (984 and 65;
          488 and 43), which raise the movement's forces-injured total from
          1,557–2,027 to 1,664–2,130.

   Arithmetic cross-checks:
     · arrest flows sum to 6,547 through 6 Oct = ministry stock 6,059 (5 Oct)
       + 488 (6 Oct) ✓;
     · forces injured, movement total = 1,664–2,130;
     · policing cost = DEPLOYMENT × RATE = €49.2M–€484.9M (cumulative high
       ≈ €485M) — unchanged.

   Flag vocabulary: n national · r regional only · p partial · f floor ·
   e derived · c cumulative stock / subset of a larger published total ·
   w weekly aggregate.                                                          */

import {
  METRICS,
  METRIC_OF,
  isDetached,
  type Band,
  type Flag,
  type Metric,
  type MetricKey,
  type NoteRef,
  type WeekNoteParts,
} from "./model.ts";

export interface Obs {
  low: number;
  high: number;
  q?: Flag;
}

export interface Pt {
  v: number | null;
  band: [number, number] | null;
  q: Flag | null;
  note: NoteRef | null;
  detach: boolean;
}
export type Series = Pt[];

const NIL: Pt = { v: null, band: null, q: null, note: null, detach: false };

export const START = "2026-09-15";
export const END = "2026-11-11";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ calendar */

const utc = (s: string): Date => new Date(`${s}T00:00:00Z`);
export const iso = (d: Date): string => d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

export const addDays = (isoDate: string, n: number): string => {
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + n);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

/** ISO date of the Monday on or before `isoDate`. */
export const mondayOf = (isoDate: string): string => {
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - ((d.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7)); // Mon→0 … Sun→6
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

function eachDay(start: string, end: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const stop = utc(end);
  for (const d = utc(start); d <= stop; d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 1))
    out.push(iso(d));
  return out;
}

export const DATES: readonly string[] = eachDay(START, END);

export interface Week {
  key: string; // ISO Monday — the aggregator key
  end: string; // ISO Sunday
  dates: string[];
  touchesEdge: boolean; // the week is cut by the window on either side
}

export const WEEKS: readonly Week[] = (() => {
  const byKey = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const d of DATES) {
    const k = mondayOf(d);
    const list = byKey.get(k);
    if (list) list.push(d);
    else byKey.set(k, [d]);
  }
  return [...byKey]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
    .map(([key, dates]) => {
      const end = addDays(key, 6);
      return { key, end, dates, touchesEdge: key < START || end > END };
    });
})();

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------- facts */

export const FACTS: Readonly<Record<string, Partial<Record<MetricKey, Obs>>>> =
  {
    /* ── The movement's first fortnight ─────────────────────────────────────
       The wave began after the rentrée: teachers and parents had been on
       strike in Mayotte since 31 August (lycée Nord d'Acoua, blocked for
       five weeks); in mainland France the lycée Théodore-Monod at Le Rheu
       was blocked on 7 September, the lycée Queneau at Villeneuve-d'Ascq on
       14 September and the lycée Victor-Hugo at Caen on 15 September. The
       window opens on 15 September at the user's request, so the 7 and 14
       September blockades fall outside it.

       NONE of 15–27 September carries a published turnout, arrest, injury or
       damage figure, so the week of 14–20 September is empty rather than
       estimated. The one exception is the Paris area on 25 September. */

    /* Fri 25 Sep — the Île-de-France centre of gravity: ~60 franciliens
       lycées disrupted over 24–25 Sep, mainly Seine-Saint-Denis and
       Val-de-Marne (Saint-Denis, Sarcelles, Gonesse, Stains, Saint-Ouen,
       Rosny-sous-Bois, La Courneuve); the Créteil rectorat counted a
       "grosse dizaine" of new blockades that day.
       21 interpellations by noon by the Préfecture de Police, in connection
       with the Paris-area mobilisations — PARIS ONLY, hence "r". The
       rectorat noted gatherings, crowd movements and material damage
       without quantifying them. */
    "2026-09-25": {
      arrests: { low: 21, high: 21, q: "r" },
    },

    /* Mon 28 Sep — the movement goes national: actions in Marseille, Lille,
       the Rhône, the Var and the Alpes-Maritimes. Interior Ministry: 180
       establishments perturbed, 27 of them totally blocked, 164
       interpellations that day.
       First serious injury of the movement: a 14-year-old hit in the face
       by a police projectile at the lycée professionnel Château-d'Epluches,
       Saint-Ouen-l'Aumône (Val-d'Oise); the IGPN was seized. Flagged "f":
       one documented casualty is a minimum, not a count. */
    "2026-09-28": {
      arrests: { low: 164, high: 164 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 1, high: 1, q: "f" },
    },

    /* Tue 29 Sep — national public-service mobilisation day, which the USL
       called students to join; it is the movement's Act I.
       Education ministry, end of day: 416 establishments touched since the
       morning, 338 still affected, 45 blocked; Le Monde counted actions at
       nearly 410 establishments; the USL claimed 400 blocked out of ~3,700
       in France. 440 interpellations, two thirds of them leading to a garde
       à vue, "essentially for degradations and attacks on officers"
       (Nuñez, BFM-TV).
       Turnout: the intersyndicale demonstration drew 206,000 (ministry) /
       300,000 (CGT) nationwide and 30,000 / 70,000 in Paris, across 170+
       demonstrations. The students marched with it, so the figure is
       carried here — but it counts the public-service cortèges, not a
       student crowd, for which no national count exists. Flagged "p".
       The Education ministry counted 31 injured students and staff that
       day. Strike rate: 11.47% weighted (12.74% second degree, 27.39%
       life-schools staff; 13.71% of teachers). */
    "2026-09-29": {
      turnout: { low: 206_000, high: 300_000, q: "p" },
      arrests: { low: 440, high: 440 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 31, high: 31, q: "p" },
    },

    /* Wed 30 Sep — 356 establishments perturbed; 625 interpellations that
       day, which with 440 on the 29th makes the two-day total 1,065.
       83 police and gendarmes injured.
       The Education ministry's stock since the start of the blockades
       reaches 119 injured students and staff (85 students, 34 staff) — a
       cumulative level, hence "c". */
    "2026-09-30": {
      arrests: { low: 625, high: 625 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 119, high: 119, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Thu 1 Oct — Acte II. More than 900 actions and blockages; AFP's police
       returns counted 340 actions in the morning and 169 blockades, ending
       the day at 900 actions including 400 closures; the Union étudiante
       claimed 640 lycées and ~30 campuses blocked, a figure the authorities
       do not confirm. Marseille: 18 high schools totally blocked, 3
       partially, ~40 in Bouches-du-Rhône; Aix-en-Provence ~600 students;
       Metz 6; Brest most; Épinal 2; Perpignan 11 interpellations and one
       student injured. No national turnout figure was published.

       FIX 5: arrests 1,949 are THAT DAY ("1 949 interpellations ce jeudi"),
       not a stock; the only stock is 6,059 "depuis le 28 septembre" on
       5 Oct. The day was the peak of the first week.

       FIX 7: forces injured are bracketed [83, 300] — 83 is the
       government level at the Wednesday cut-off (it is the same 83 that
       appears in the 318-injury total of 1 Oct) and ~300 is Nuñez's
       Thursday figure ("quelque 300 policiers et gendarmes blessés en
       intervention jeudi"). The band spans a cumulative level and a daily
       flow, so it is flagged "e" and the 83 is not counted twice.

       FIX 6: 235 = 170 students + 65 education staff, cumulative at the
       1 Oct cut-off; the previous 119–235 band was two vintages of this
       same series. Flagged "c".

       Damage: the hall of the lycée Nelson-Mandela in Nantes was destroyed
       by a fire (several classrooms unusable for weeks), as was the main
       entrance and a 35-classroom block at the lycée Henri-Moissan in
       Meaux; the façade of the lycée Lumière in Lyon was burnt; in
       Seine-Maritime two headteachers were hurt (Petit-Quevilly, Rouen) and
       the prefecture counted eight injured and several intrusions. The €1M
       cell is a local estimate from the original research and is a subset
       of the larger Île-de-France and IFRAP totals, hence "c". */
    "2026-10-01": {
      arrests: { low: 1_949, high: 1_949 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 83, high: 300, q: "e" },
      protesterInjured: { low: 235, high: 235, q: "c" },
      damagesCost: { low: 1, high: 1, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Fri 2 Oct — 1,747 interpellations that day and "more than 5,000 since
       Monday" (the daily series gives 4,925); 735 establishments perturbed,
       30% fewer than the day before, with urban violence near 158 of them
       (projectiles, attacks on officers, teachers, journalists and
       passers-by). A young police lieutenant was assaulted in Paris's 20th
       arrondissement. Geffray: 65 education staff injured since the start
       of the movement, 40 of them headteachers. */
    "2026-10-02": {
      arrests: { low: 1_747, high: 1_747 },
    },

    /* Sat 3 Oct — the ministry's stock "6,059 interpellations since
       28 September" (5 Oct) minus 984 on Monday 5 Oct leaves 5,075 through
       Sunday 4 Oct; the daily figures for 28 Sep–2 Oct sum to 4,925. The
       difference, 150, is the weekend of 3–4 October. It is a two-day total
       derived by subtraction, not a Saturday figure — flagged "e" and
       carried on the Saturday, with the derivation in this comment. */
    "2026-10-03": {
      arrests: { low: 150, high: 150, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Mon 5 Oct — 984 interpellations and 65 forces injured that day.
       Stocks published the same day: 6,059 interpellations since 28 Sep;
       715 police and gendarmes injured since the start of the movement;
       Matignon: 215 students and 85 education staff injured since the
       start, 34 of them headteachers. */
    "2026-10-05": {
      arrests: { low: 984, high: 984 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 65, high: 65 },
    },

    /* Tue 6 Oct — "Acte III", first national school/student day.
       256,000 ministry / 450,000 organisers; Paris 56,000 / 100,000.
       488 interpellations and 43 forces injured that day; 215 students
       injured since mid-September. The €6M is the Île-de-France damage
       increment for the 1–8 Oct run and is therefore a subset, not an
       increment unique to 6 Oct. Flagged "c"; the anchor of the run is
       8 Oct. */
    "2026-10-06": {
      turnout: { low: 256_000, high: 450_000 },
      arrests: { low: 488, high: 488 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 43, high: 43 },
      damagesCost: { low: 6, high: 6, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Thu 8 Oct — "Acte IV": 71,500 ministry / 130,000+ CGT; Paris 24,500.
       Anchor of the 1–8 Oct Île-de-France damage increment (€6M). */
    "2026-10-08": {
      turnout: { low: 71_500, high: 130_000 },
      damagesCost: { low: 6, high: 6, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Mon 12 Oct — regional only: 13 interpellations in Rhône. */
    "2026-10-12": { arrests: { low: 13, high: 13, q: "r" } },

    /* Tue 13 Oct — "Acte V": 214 high schools closed (94.2% open).
       161,500 ministry / 480,000 youth intersyndicate; Paris 41,500.
       40 police and gendarmes injured that day; cumulative since the start of
       the movement: 937 police, 248 students, 149 staff.
       Regional check (Préfecture de l'Ardèche, point de situation 17 h):
       Privas 130, Annonay 120, Tournon 60, Aubenas 600 including students;
       100+ police mobilised; no establishment blocked, no violence, no
       damage, two procedures for insults against officers. */
    "2026-10-13": {
      turnout: { low: 161_500, high: 480_000 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 40, high: 40 },
      damagesCost: { low: 1.5, high: 1.5, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Sat 17 Oct — the peak of the episode.
       110 → 430 → 441 actions; ~60,000 officers deployed.
       550,000 ministry / 800,000 organisers; Paris 110,000.
       Arrests 2,397 initial → 2,628 consolidated (2,513 gardes à vue;
       108 in Paris). Forces injured 997 initial → 1,144 consolidated,
       7 serious. One civilian death: Christophe M., 59, Paris.

       FIX 2 (first pass): no official protester-injury count exists. The
       low bound is Laurent Thines's serious-injury register (54 seriously
       injured — 5 eye, 5 cranio-facial, 2 amputations, 42 other — plus the
       1 death, i.e. 55); the high bound is a reasoned ceiling from
       Toulouse's 130+ street-medics treatments (~13 treated per serious
       case) against 54 serious cases nationally. Wikipedia's "400+ civilians"
       is an uncited infobox line and sits below the partial nine-city sum
       (~204 across Bordeaux, Clermont-Ferrand, Dijon, Metz, Nancy, Rouen,
       Strasbourg, Avignon, Toulouse), so it is a floor, not a ceiling.
       Flagged "e": derived, drawn hollow.

       Damages: IFRAP conditional estimate for the 17–18 Oct weekend,
       €244M low / €463M central / €866M high for MATERIAL damage; the
       €294M–€1.1bn variant includes lost turnover and is a different measure.
       IFRAP excludes marginal order-maintenance and curfew costs. */
    "2026-10-17": {
      turnout: { low: 550_000, high: 800_000 },
      arrests: { low: 2_397, high: 2_628 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 997, high: 1_144 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 55, high: 1000, q: "e" },
      damagesCost: { low: 244, high: 866, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Sun 18 Oct — Paris curfew only, 108 interpellations.
       FIX 3 (first pass): the €1.8–5M damage figure is a subset of the
       17–18 Oct IFRAP weekend total, so it is flagged "c" and cannot be
       added to it. */
    "2026-10-18": {
      arrests: { low: 108, high: 108, q: "p" },
      damagesCost: { low: 1.8, high: 5, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Tue 20 Oct — regional only: 13 interpellations overnight in Guadeloupe
       (Basse-Terre, Saint-François, Goyave, Vieux-Habitants, Gosier). */
    "2026-10-20": { arrests: { low: 13, high: 13, q: "r" } },

    /* Sat 24 Oct — 289 actions, 70,000 officers, Centaure AFVs, water cannons,
       14 aerial cameras in Paris. 410,000 ministry / 800,000+ CGT;
       Paris 110,000 (préfecture) / 250,000 (CGT).
       Arrests 1,314 initial → 1,435 final (142 Paris, 126 GàV).
       Forces injured 53 at 7:30 a.m. — the ministry's own communiqué calls
       this "provisoire" — then 267 on consolidation (Nuñez, 26 Oct), with
       150 civilians injured, 3 seriously. 75 fire interventions.
       La Réunion: ~80 Saint-Denis, 300 Saint-Pierre. Mayotte: Tsingoni.
       FIX 4 (first pass): the national damage cell below replaces the earlier
       local derivation. CCI France's 27 Oct tally — 2,488 shops hit after the
       17th plus 50 after the 24th, 1,161 heavily damaged, 643 still shut —
       puts the cost of the degradations at over €1bn on the IFRAP estimate,
       for the 17–24 Oct period as a whole. That is a SUPERSET of the 17 Oct
       cell, so it is flagged "c" and is never added to it. */
    "2026-10-24": {
      turnout: { low: 410_000, high: 800_000 },
      arrests: { low: 1_314, high: 1_435 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 267, high: 267 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 150, high: 150 },
      damagesCost: { low: 1_000, high: 1_000, q: "c" },
    },

    /* Sat 31 Oct — 291 actions → 227 consolidated.
       320,000 ministry / ~500,000 youth organisations; Paris 70,000.
       Arrests 565 at 21h → 779 cumulative (incl. 214 on the Halloween night).
       Forces injured 116 by 21h + 43 that night = 159.
       515 fires handled; 36 shops degraded, 3 pillaged — no cost figure. */
    "2026-10-31": {
      turnout: { low: 320_000, high: 500_000 },
      arrests: { low: 565, high: 779 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 116, high: 159 },
      damagesCost: { low: 0.4, high: 3.3, q: "e" },
    },

    /* Thu 5 Nov — national strike day, 193 actions.
       575,000 ministry (definitive) / 1.6M CGT; Paris 65,000 / 350,000.
       Arrests 448 at 18h → 642 updated. Forces injured 53 at 18h → 112.
       86 fire interventions. Marseille: 17 schools blocked, ~40 interpellations,
       13 police wounded.
       Protester injuries = 18, a FLOOR: the 18 who instructed lawyers to sue
       Nuñez. Not a ministry count. */
    "2026-11-05": {
      turnout: { low: 575_000, high: 1_600_000 },
      arrests: { low: 448, high: 642 },
      forcesInjured: { low: 53, high: 112 },
      protesterInjured: { low: 18, high: 18, q: "f" },
    },

    /* Sat 7 Nov — 141 actions; ~70,000 officers, 25 blindés, 15 water-cannon
       trucks; ~40,000 preventive checks; Caen campus dispersed by 50 CRS.
       146,000 ministry / 250,000 youth intersyndicate; Paris 46,000 / 75,000.
       Arrests 232 at 18h → 360+ at 19h.
       Security-force casualties acknowledged but no number published. */
    "2026-11-07": {
      turnout: { low: 146_000, high: 250_000 },
      arrests: { low: 350, high: 360 },
    },

    /* Mon 9 Nov — regional only: Bègles 4, Lyon 8, Strasbourg 5 = 17.
       ~50 Île-de-France schools disrupted with mortar fire; Rennes 2
       evacuated; Brest rally; Guadeloupe: lycée blocked at Baie-Mahault. */
    "2026-11-09": { arrests: { low: 17, high: 17, q: "r" } },
  };

export const DEPLOYMENT: Readonly<Record<string, Obs>> = {
  /* September deployment is an ASSUMPTION, on the same basis as the early
     October cells: no official headcount exists for these days. The band
     spans the marginal cost of one CRS company-day to the Sainte-Soline
     all-in benchmark. It is an order-of-magnitude placeholder for the
     policing-cost series, not a measurement. */
  "2026-09-28": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-09-29": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-09-30": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-01": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-02": { low: 10_000, high: 30_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-05": { low: 10_000, high: 30_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-06": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-08": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-12": { low: 2_000, high: 8_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-13": { low: 5_000, high: 20_000, q: "e" },
  "2026-10-17": { low: 60_000, high: 60_000 },
  "2026-10-24": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
  "2026-10-31": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
  "2026-11-05": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
  "2026-11-07": { low: 70_000, high: 70_000 },
};

/** € per officer per day: CRS-company marginal opex → Sainte-Soline all-in.
 *  Low  = marginal cost of one CRS company-day (overtime, travel, fuel,
 *         accommodation, munitions). Salaries are excluded because officers
 *         are paid anyway.
 *  High = 2023 Sainte-Soline benchmark: ~3,000 gendarmes ≈ €3.4M → €1,133.
 *  Equipment context: grenade €36–75, LBD Monocup €1,280, Centaure €80,000.
 *  Yields a movement total of €49.2M–€484.9M (cumulative high ≈ €485M). */
export const RATE = { low: 136, high: 1_133 } as const;

const round2 = (v: number): number =>
  Number.isInteger(v) ? v : Math.round(v * 100) / 100;

const costBand = (dep: Obs): [number, number] => [
  (dep.low * RATE.low) / 1e6,
  (dep.high * RATE.high) / 1e6,
];

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- projections */

function projectDaily(key: MetricKey): Series {
  return DATES.map((date) => {
    const obs = FACTS[date]?.[key];
    if (!obs) return NIL;
    const q = obs.q ?? "n";
    return {
      v: round2((obs.low + obs.high) / 2),
      band: [obs.low, obs.high] as [number, number],
      q,
      note: { k: "obs", date, metric: key } as NoteRef,
      detach: isDetached(q),
    };
  });
}

function projectPolicingDaily(): Series {
  return DATES.map((date) => {
    const dep = DEPLOYMENT[date];
    if (!dep) return NIL;
    const [lo, hi] = costBand(dep);
    const q = dep.q ?? "n";
    return {
      v: round2((lo + hi) / 2),
      band: [round2(lo), round2(hi)] as [number, number],
      q,
      note: { k: "policing", low: dep.low, high: dep.high, date } as NoteRef,
      detach: isDetached(q),
    };
  });
}

/** Running total; carries forward on days with no deployment — a total does not fall. */
function projectPolicingCumulative(): Series {
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = 0;
  let days = 0;
  let started = false;

  return DATES.map((date) => {
    const dep = DEPLOYMENT[date];
    if (dep) {
      const [a, b] = costBand(dep);
      lo += a;
      hi += b;
      days++;
      started = true;
    }
    if (!started) return NIL;
    return {
      v: round2((lo + hi) / 2),
      band: [round2(lo), round2(hi)] as [number, number],
      q: "e",
      note: { k: "cumulative", days, added: Boolean(dep) } as NoteRef,
      detach: false, // always a line; its band is shaded, not barred
    };
  });
}

/* ── Aggregation rules (rewritten in the second pass, 12 Nov 2026) ────────
   1. A "c" cell is a cumulative level or a subset of a larger published
      total. It is never added to a flow.
   2. If a week's LAST dated cell for a metric is a stock, that stock IS the
      week's figure — a level is a stronger statement than a partial sum, and
      summing flows into it would double-count. The week is flagged "c" and
      detached. This is what carries the week of 28 Sep, whose injury figure
      is the 235 cumulative level reached on 1 Oct.
   3. Otherwise the week's flows are summed. Regional-only ("r") cells are
      excluded whenever a national-scope cell exists for the same week, and
      any earlier stock cell is excluded too (it is reported in its own
      daily cell). */
function aggregate(key: MetricKey, op: "sum" | "max"): Series {
  return WEEKS.map((week) => {
    const cells: { date: string; obs: Obs; q: Flag }[] = [];
    for (const d of week.dates) {
      const obs = FACTS[d]?.[key];
      if (!obs) continue;
      cells.push({ date: d, obs, q: obs.q ?? "n" });
    }
    if (!cells.length) return NIL;

    const last = cells[cells.length - 1];

    /* Rule 2 — the week's last cell is a cumulative level: it wins. */
    if (last.q === "c") {
      return {
        v: round2((last.obs.low + last.obs.high) / 2),
        band: [round2(last.obs.low), round2(last.obs.high)] as [number, number],
        q: "c",
        note: {
          k: "week",
          p: { days: cells.length, kind: "national" },
        } as NoteRef,
        detach: true,
      };
    }

    /* Rule 3 — split the remaining cells. */
    const regional = cells.filter((c) => c.q === "r");
    const flows = cells.filter((c) => c.q !== "c" && c.q !== "r");
    const regionalOnly = flows.length === 0 && regional.length > 0;
    const pool = regionalOnly ? regional : flows;
    if (!pool.length) return NIL;

    let low: number;
    let high: number;
    let value: number;
    let peak: string | undefined;

    if (op === "sum") {
      low = pool.reduce((a, x) => a + x.obs.low, 0);
      high = pool.reduce((a, x) => a + x.obs.high, 0);
      value = (low + high) / 2;
    } else {
      low = Math.max(...pool.map((x) => x.obs.low));
      high = Math.max(...pool.map((x) => x.obs.high));
      const top = pool.reduce((best, x) =>
        (x.obs.low + x.obs.high) / 2 > (best.obs.low + best.obs.high) / 2
          ? x
          : best,
      );
      value = (top.obs.low + top.obs.high) / 2;
      peak = top.date;
    }

    const excluded = cells.length - pool.length;
    const partial = pool.some((x) => x.obs.q === "p");
    const parts: WeekNoteParts = {
      days: pool.length,
      kind: regionalOnly ? "regional" : "national",
      peak,
      noNational: regionalOnly || undefined,
      excluded: excluded > 0 ? excluded : undefined,
      floored: pool.some((x) => x.obs.q === "f") || undefined,
      clipped: week.touchesEdge || undefined,
    };

    return {
      v: round2(value),
      band: [round2(low), round2(high)] as [number, number],
      q: regionalOnly ? "r" : parts.floored ? "f" : partial ? "p" : "w",
      note: { k: "week", p: parts } as NoteRef,
      detach: regionalOnly,
    };
  });
}

export const DAILY: Readonly<Record<string, Series>> = {
  turnout: projectDaily("turnout"),
  arrests: projectDaily("arrests"),
  forcesInjured: projectDaily("forcesInjured"),
  protesterInjured: projectDaily("protesterInjured"),
  damagesCost: projectDaily("damagesCost"),
  policing: projectPolicingDaily(),
  policingCumulative: projectPolicingCumulative(),
};

export const WEEKLY: Readonly<Record<string, Series>> = {
  turnout: aggregate("turnout", "max"),
  arrests: aggregate("arrests", "sum"),
  forcesInjured: aggregate("forcesInjured", "sum"),
  protesterInjured: aggregate("protesterInjured", "sum"),
};

/* ----------------------------------------------- section C's weekly table */

export interface StudentWeek {
  key: string; // ISO Monday — also the row's `from`
  end: string; // ISO Sunday
  touchesEdge: boolean;
  p: [number, number] | null;
  a: [number, number] | null;
  f: [number, number] | null;
  i: [number, number] | null;
  flags: Partial<Record<Metric, Flag>>;
  notes: Partial<Record<Metric, NoteRef>>;
}

/** Editorial corrections the aggregator cannot infer from a band alone.
 *  Keyed by ISO Monday, so inserting a fact above cannot shift them. */
const WEEK_FLAG_OVERRIDE: Partial<
  Record<string, Partial<Record<Metric, Flag>>>
> = {
  // Week of 28 Sep: the injury figure is the cumulative level reached on
  // 1 Oct (235 students and staff). The cell-level "c" flag produces this
  // on its own; the override documents the intent and survives any future
  // re-ordering of the underlying cells.
  "2026-09-28": { i: "c" },
};

export const STUDENT_WEEKS: readonly StudentWeek[] = WEEKS.map((week, idx) => {
  const out: StudentWeek = {
    key: week.key,
    end: week.end,
    touchesEdge: week.touchesEdge,
    p: null,
    a: null,
    f: null,
    i: null,
    flags: {},
    notes: {},
  };
  const override = WEEK_FLAG_OVERRIDE[week.key];

  for (const m of METRICS) {
    const pt = WEEKLY[METRIC_OF[m]][idx];
    if (pt.band) out[m] = pt.band;
    const q = override?.[m] ?? pt.q;
    // A flag is only meaningful where there is a band: an empty week must
    // not inherit an override and sprout a phantom marker.
    if (pt.band && q && q !== "n") out.flags[m] = q;
    if (pt.note) out.notes[m] = pt.note;
  }
  return out;
});

/* ── Still outstanding (no figure currently in the table) ───────────────────
   1. 6 Oct and 8 Oct: the €6M Île-de-France increment has one anchor (8 Oct);
      the 1 Oct €1M cell is part of the same run. Confirm whether 1 Oct's €1M
      is an independent estimate or part of that increment.
   2. 24 Oct: the ministry's provisional forces-injured count for that day is
      53 in the audit trail, later consolidated to 267. The table keeps the
      consolidated 267 (it reproduces the 1,664–2,130 movement total);
      a 53–267 band should be adopted if the 18 h communiqué confirms 53.
   3. 17 Oct: no official protester-injury count exists. The Thines register
      stood at 79 cases on 5 Nov and was still rising; the band should be
      re-cut from the latest register.
   4. Policing cost for 28 Sep–8 Oct rests on a derived 5,000–30,000
      deployment assumption, not on published headcounts. No official
      headcount exists for any September day.
   5. 15–24 Sep: no turnout, arrest, injury or damage figure exists for any
      date. The week of 14 Sep is therefore empty rather than estimated.
      The 21 interpellations of 25 Sep are PARIS ONLY (Préfecture de
      Police, by noon) and are the only number before the 28 Sep national
      extension.
   6. 29 Sep: the only turnout figures (206,000 ministry / 300,000 CGT) are
      for the intersyndicale public-service cortège that the lycée actions
      joined; no student-only crowd count was ever published.
   7. 3–4 Oct: the weekend's 150 arrests are derived by subtraction from
      the ministry's "6,059 since 28 September" (5 Oct). The ministry never
      published a weekend figure.
   8. 13 Oct: the Ardèche prefecture figures (Privas 130, Annonay 120,
      Tournon 60, Aubenas 600) are regional and are deliberately not entered
      as a second turnout cell for that date. */
```

---

## `episodes.ts`

```ts
/* Section C — cross-episode comparison.
   Primary figure: one panel per episode, four series per panel, one shared log axis.
   Companion: one panel per series, one line per episode on a sequential ramp.

   ── Verification pass, 12 Nov 2026 ─────────────────────────────────────────
   Every p/a/f/i cell below was checked against the sourced weekly tables in
   "French Protest Charts" Part II and, for the 2026 episode, against the
   ministry's September–October bilans. Numeric changes since the previous
   pass: the 2026 episode now opens on 14 September 2026 (nine weekly rows
   instead of seven) and carries the Act I figures; the pensions episode
   gains two enforcement rows; the Nahel p band was replaced by a sourced
   one; the Gilets jaunes enforcement cells were replaced by per-act figures
   that match the published source.

   Convention in this file: p = protestors at the PEAK DAY of the week;
   a/f/i = weekly totals. Bands are authority (low) → organiser (high) where
   an organiser count exists; otherwise a point value. */

import {
  Chart,
  type ChartData,
  type ChartDataset,
  type ChartOptions,
  type TooltipItem,
} from "chart.js";

import { compact, fill, fmtSpan, fmtWeek, nf, plural, t } from "./i18n.ts";
import { STUDENT_WEEKS, type StudentWeek } from "./data.ts";
import {
  METRICS,
  bandOf,
  isDetached,
  midOf,
  type Episode,
  type EpisodeId,
  type Flag,
  type Metric,
  type Row,
} from "./model.ts";
import { THEME, alpha, gapSegment, track } from "./canvas.ts";
import { noteText } from "./notes.ts";
import { el } from "./dom.ts";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------- types */

interface Pt {
  x: number;
  y: number; // midpoint of the published range — this is what gets plotted
  low: number;
  high: number;
  flag: Flag;
  note?: string;
  base: number;
  t: string;
}

interface PanelDataset extends ChartDataset<"line", Pt[]> {
  __item: Metric;
  __ep: number;
  __color: string;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- constants */

const DIM = THEME.dim;

/** Colour only — labels come from t().items. */
const SERIES: readonly { key: Metric; color: string }[] = [
  { key: "p", color: "#0072B2" },
  { key: "a", color: "#E69F00" },
  { key: "f", color: "#009E73" },
  { key: "i", color: "#CC79A7" },
];

/** Sequential ramp, light → dark, identical in every companion panel. */
const RAMP = [
  "#D6E6F2",
  "#B0CFE6",
  "#86B4D7",
  "#5C97C5",
  "#357AB0",
  "#1B5C92",
  "#0A3A63",
] as const;

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------- data */

/* ── May 1968 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The street crowd was measured ad hoc: prefecture counts, "surface occupée"
   estimates and union claims. Only 1, 6, 10 and 13 May carry a crowd figure.
   The general strike (up to ~10M workers on 25 May) is a DIFFERENT population
   from the march and is deliberately excluded from the p series.
   The 30 May counter-demonstration (300,000 prefecture / 1M Gaullists) is a
   pro-government rally and is likewise excluded from p.                   */
const MAY68: Episode = {
  id: "may68",
  start: "1968-04-29",
  end: "1968-06-30",
  weeks: [
    /* 1 May, République → Bastille: 25,000 (police) vs 100,000 (CGT). */
    { w: 0, from: "1968-04-29", to: "1968-05-05", p: [25_000, 100_000] },
    {
      /* 6 May: >20,000 to the Sorbonne; 10 May: 20,000 (12,000 police).
         Night of 10–11 May: 6,255 police, 125 cars damaged / 63 burnt,
         469 arrests. The 891 high adds 422 early-May arrests.
         Forces: 300+ on 6 May, 247 on 10–11 May.
         Protesters injured: ≥100 — no national count was published. */
      w: 1,
      from: "1968-05-06",
      to: "1968-05-12",
      p: 20_000,
      a: [469, 891],
      f: [247, 300],
      i: 100,
      flag: { i: "f" },
    },
    /* 13 May, Paris — peak of the episode: prefecture 230,000 · ORTF 171,000
       · surface-area estimate 500,000 · unions 1,000,000. */
    { w: 2, from: "1968-05-13", to: "1968-05-19", p: [230_000, 1_000_000] },
    {
      /* 25 May: 1,000+ arrests (floor), ~9M strikers, ORTF walkout.
         887 injured — from the English-Wikipedia infobox, attributed to 25 May
         only. Weak provenance, flagged "e". */
      w: 3,
      from: "1968-05-20",
      to: "1968-05-26",
      a: 1_000,
      i: 887,
      flag: { a: "f", i: "e" },
    },
    /* 27 May Grenelle protocol · Charléty meeting. */
    { w: 4, from: "1968-05-27", to: "1968-06-02" },
    /* 4 Jun partial return to work · 7 Jun Renault Flins clashes. */
    { w: 5, from: "1968-06-03", to: "1968-06-09" },
    /* 10 Jun: Tautin killed · 11 Jun: 2 killed at Sochaux · 12 Jun:
       demonstrations banned nationwide. */
    { w: 6, from: "1968-06-10", to: "1968-06-16" },
    /* 23 Jun legislative elections. */
    { w: 7, from: "1968-06-17", to: "1968-06-23" },
    /* Crisis closes 30 Jun. */
    { w: 8, from: "1968-06-24", to: "1968-06-30" },
  ],
};

/* ── CPE 2006 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The worst-documented episode for enforcement: the CAJ total (4,500 arrests,
   1,950 in garde à vue, 635 prosecutions) is movement-wide, not a per-day
   series, and it counts arrests rather than custody. The Paris-only figures
   for 18 March are the most reliable single-day numbers in the episode.
   OPEN GAP (closed as "no such figure exists"): 15 Feb, 25 Feb, 4 Mar and
   11 Mar action days carry no crowd figure in either encyclopaedic source;
   the only series published is 7 Feb, 16 Mar, 28 Mar and 4 Apr. One addition
   was made: 14 Mar 2006 = 41,000 (police source), a second action day
   inside the week of 16 Mar.                                         */
const CPE: Episode = {
  id: "cpe",
  start: "2006-02-06",
  end: "2006-04-16",
  weeks: [
    /* 7 Feb: first national action day, 187 demonstrations. */
    { w: 0, from: "2006-02-06", to: "2006-02-12", p: [200_000, 400_000] },
    /* 7 Mar: ">1 million" claimed; ~38 universities on strike by 9 Mar. */
    { w: 4, from: "2006-03-06", to: "2006-03-12", p: [400_000, 1_000_000] },
    {
      /* 16 Mar: 250,000 (Le Monde) / 447,500 (police) — the ministry's
         247,500 is the same police count rounded differently.
         18 Mar: 500,000 ministry / 1.5M organisers; Paris 80,000–350,000.
         Enforcement, Paris only: 156 (ministry) / 167 (organisers) arrests;
         34 forces injured.
         Protesters injured 70 = 18 (16 Mar) + 52 (18 Mar), Paris only —
         flagged "e" because it is a two-day Paris sum inside a weekly row.
         14 Mar (same week, second action day): 41,000 (police source). */
      w: 5,
      from: "2006-03-13",
      to: "2006-03-19",
      p: [500_000, 1_500_000],
      a: [156, 167],
      f: 34,
      i: 70,
      flag: { i: "e" },
    },
    {
      /* 23 Mar: 21 universities fully blocked, 67 striking or blocked.
         630 arrests, 90 forces injured. */
      w: 6,
      from: "2006-03-20",
      to: "2006-03-26",
      p: [220_000, 450_000],
      a: 630,
      f: 90,
    },
    /* 28 Mar: 4th national action day, 600 arrests. */
    { w: 7, from: "2006-03-27", to: "2006-04-02", p: [1_055_000, 2_710_000], a: 600 },
    /* 4 Apr: peak day of the episode. */
    { w: 8, from: "2006-04-03", to: "2006-04-09", p: [1_000_000, 3_100_000] },
    /* CPE scrapped 10 Apr; the movement splits, Easter follows. */
    { w: 9, from: "2006-04-10", to: "2006-04-16" },
  ],
};

/* ── Gilets jaunes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   One Ministry count per Saturday act, so no range is plotted: the episode is
   deliberately a ministry series. Acts 34–43 were published as "NC" (not
   communicated) and Acts 45–52 were never quantified — absence of a figure,
   not absence of people.
   Act I's 287,700 counts ALL actions including road blockades, so it is an
   actions-total rather than a march count: flagged "c", hollow, and excluded
   from the index baseline.
   Act II was initially published as 106,301 and revised up 8 days later.     */
const GJ_ACTS: readonly [number, string, number][] = [
  [0, "2018-11-17", 287_700],
  [1, "2018-11-24", 166_000],
  [2, "2018-12-01", 136_000],
  [3, "2018-12-08", 136_000],
  [4, "2018-12-15", 66_000],
  [5, "2018-12-22", 38_600],
  [6, "2018-12-29", 32_000],
  [7, "2019-01-05", 50_000],
  [8, "2019-01-12", 84_000],
  [9, "2019-01-19", 84_000],
  [10, "2019-01-26", 69_000],
  [11, "2019-02-02", 58_600],
  [12, "2019-02-09", 51_400],
  [13, "2019-02-16", 41_500],
  [14, "2019-02-23", 46_600],
  [15, "2019-03-02", 39_300],
  [16, "2019-03-09", 28_600],
  [17, "2019-03-16", 32_300],
  [18, "2019-03-23", 40_500],
  [19, "2019-03-30", 33_700],
  [20, "2019-04-06", 22_300],
  [21, "2019-04-13", 31_000],
  [22, "2019-04-20", 27_900],
  [23, "2019-04-27", 23_600],
  [24, "2019-05-04", 18_900],
  [25, "2019-05-11", 18_600],
  [26, "2019-05-18", 15_500],
  [27, "2019-05-25", 12_500],
  [28, "2019-06-01", 9_500],
  [29, "2019-06-08", 10_300],
  [30, "2019-06-15", 7_000],
  [31, "2019-06-22", 11_800],
  [32, "2019-06-29", 5_800],
  [43, "2019-09-07", 7_000],
];

/** Organiser / union counter-counts for the Gilets jaunes acts, published
 *  alongside the ministry figure but NOT plotted (the episode is a ministry
 *  series). Exported so the data is auditable and so a band version can be
 *  switched on without re-research: a band version would make this episode
 *  comparable with the others, which use authority–organiser ranges.
 *  Source: FR-Wikipedia "Déroulement du mouvement des Gilets jaunes" and
 *  Ouest-France's running count of the mobilisation. */
export const GJ_ORGANISER_COUNTS: Readonly<Record<number, number>> = {
  1: 750_000, // France Police union, Act II
  7: 123_400, // Act VIII,  5 Jan 2019
  8: 159_200, // Act IX,  12 Jan 2019
  9: 147_400, // Act X,   19 Jan 2019
  10: 123_200, // Act XI,  26 Jan 2019
  11: 116_000, // Act XII,  2 Feb 2019
  13: 104_100, // Act XIV, 16 Feb 2019
};

/** Enforcement figures exist only for Acts I–IV; later acts were not published
 *  as a series.
 *  Act I: ministry communiqué — peak 287,710 at 17:00, 3,000 sites occupied,
 *    1 death, 409 injured, 117 interpellations of which 73 GàV. The 409 is
 *    all sides and is NOT split, so no f/i pair is plotted.
 *  Act II: 24 injured including 5 police; 101 arrestations (ministry) or
 *    103 interpellations (Le JDD) for the same day. The 19 protester
 *    injuries are DERIVED (24 − 5), hence "e".
 *  Act III: Arc de Triomphe vandalism; no per-act totals published.
 *  Act IV: 1,385 interpellations and 974 GàV; ~89,000 agents mobilised —
 *    the largest enforcement operation of the episode. */
const GJ_EXTRA: Readonly<Record<number, Pick<Row, "a" | "f" | "i" | "flag">>> =
  {
    0: { a: [117, 73], flag: { a: "f" } },
    1: { a: [101, 103], f: 5, i: 19, flag: { i: "e" } },
    2: {},
    3: { a: 1_385 },
  };

const GILETS: Episode = {
  id: "gj",
  start: "2018-11-12",
  end: "2019-09-09",
  weeks: GJ_ACTS.map(([w, date, n]) => ({
    w,
    from: date,
    to: date,
    act: w + 1,
    p: n,
    ...(GJ_EXTRA[w] ?? {}),
    flag: {
      ...(w === 0 ? { p: "c" as Flag } : {}),
      ...(GJ_EXTRA[w]?.flag ?? {}),
    },
  })),
};

/* ── Nahel 2023 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Under-counted by march numbers: most gatherings were banned, and the crowd
   figure that exists describes the tribute march, not the riot. The riot's
   scale is carried by arrests, burnt vehicles and the Senate's ~50,000
   "émeutiers" estimate for the whole episode.
   Week 0 = 27 Jun – 2 Jul: 3,354 arrests, 722 officers injured, 2 deaths
   (Brahim Bouarram 27 Jun; a young man 1 Jul), 5,662 vehicles burnt, 1,029
   buildings damaged.
   Week 1 = 3–9 Jul: 108 arrests (72 + 16 + 20 over 3–5 Jul), 1 officer hurt.
   Note the two totals differ between sources: the English-Wikipedia infobox
   (5,662 vehicles, 3,300+ arrests, 808 officers injured, €650M) and the
   French-Wikipedia day table (5,954 vehicles, 3,462 arrests, 723 officers
   injured) do not match; the figures used here are the day-table ones.
   The p band is an EPISODE-LEVEL rioter estimate: NO crowd count exists for
   any riot night, and the Place de la République demonstration in the
   sources was called for 8 July. Darmanin 8,000–12,000; Senate committee
   ~50,000; Brochand 100,000–200,000 — the band uses the Senate figure as
   its high, so the high is conservative. Flagged "e". */
const NAHEL: Episode = {
  id: "nahel",
  start: "2023-06-27",
  end: "2023-07-09",
  weeks: [
    {
      w: 0,
      from: "2023-06-26",
      to: "2023-07-02",
      p: [8_000, 50_000],
      a: 3_354,
      f: 722,
      flag: { p: "e" },
    },
    { w: 1, from: "2023-07-03", to: "2023-07-09", a: 108, f: 1 },
  ],
};

/* ── Retirement reform 2023 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Crowds only: no national weekly arrest or injury series was published for
   this movement. Two action days in one week (7 and 11 Feb) means the
   "peak day" convention understates it — the real unit is the action-day
   sequence. The union total runs about 2.8× the ministry total, the widest
   organiser/authority gap of any episode in this file.
   14 March was the CALL; the action day was 15 March (480,000 ministry /
   1.78M CGT), already in w8. The missing enforcement data belongs to
   23 Mar (457 arrests, 441 forces injured) and 6 Apr (111 arrests, 154
   police injured). Both are now plotted — they are the only enforcement
   data points this episode has.
   OPEN GAP: the w7 band (1.28M–3.5M) has an unsourced high; the sourced
   figures for that week are 11 Mar ≈368,000 and "early March" 1.1–1.4M.   */
const PENSIONS: Episode = {
  id: "pensions",
  start: "2023-01-19",
  end: "2023-06-08",
  weeks: [
    { w: 0, from: "2023-01-16", to: "2023-01-22", p: [1_120_000, 2_000_000] },
    { w: 2, from: "2023-01-30", to: "2023-02-05", p: [1_270_000, 2_800_000] },
    { w: 3, from: "2023-02-06", to: "2023-02-12", p: [963_000, 2_500_000] },
    { w: 4, from: "2023-02-13", to: "2023-02-19", p: [440_000, 1_300_000] },
    { w: 7, from: "2023-03-06", to: "2023-03-12", p: [1_280_000, 3_500_000] },
    { w: 8, from: "2023-03-13", to: "2023-03-19", p: [480_000, 1_700_000] },
    {
      /* 23 Mar: the flashpoint — 49.3 passed, two failed no-confidence votes.
         Ministry: up to 1.08M nationwide / 119,000 Paris; CGT 3.5M / 800,000.
         Darmanin's consolidated day: 457 arrests, 441 security forces injured.
         An earlier evening statement had given 80+ arrests / 123 police hurt,
         so 457/441 is the final figure and the only one plotted. */
      w: 9,
      from: "2023-03-20",
      to: "2023-03-26",
      p: [1_089_000, 3_500_000],
      a: 457,
      f: 441,
    },
    {
      /* 27 Mar: the eleventh day of mobilisation, after the 5 April talks
         failed. Expected 600,000–800,000 nationwide (60,000–90,000 Paris).
         Interior Ministry: 111 arrests, 154 police officers injured. */
      w: 11,
      from: "2023-04-03",
      to: "2023-04-09",
      p: [570_000, 2_000_000],
      a: 111,
      f: 154,
    },
    { w: 10, from: "2023-03-27", to: "2023-04-02", p: [740_000, 2_000_000] },
    { w: 12, from: "2023-04-10", to: "2023-04-16", p: [380_000, 1_500_000] },
    { w: 16, from: "2023-05-01", to: "2023-05-07", p: [782_000, 2_300_000] },
    { w: 20, from: "2023-06-05", to: "2023-06-08", p: [281_000, 900_000] },
  ],
};

/* ── Bloquons tout 2025 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The label first appears on social media in May 2025; the first national
   action was 10 September 2025 against the Bayrou budget.
   10 Sep: the ministry's figure counts PARTICIPANTS IN ACTIONS, not cortèges —
   the same methodological trap as Gilets jaunes Act I — so it is flagged "c".
     It was also revised up from an early-evening count of ~29,000 to
     175,000–197,000; CGT ~250,000. 596 rallies, 253 blockages, 540 arrests
     (211 in Paris).
   18 Sep: an intersyndicale strike day — a different mobilisation from the
     movement's own actions. 309 arrests nationwide (103 in Paris, 89 GàV),
     26 forces injured, 11 protesters injured including 1 journalist.
   2 Oct: weakest mobilisation since 1 May 2025.
   w2 (22–28 Sep) is deliberately EMPTY: the movement ran three actions only
   — 10 Sep, 18 Sep and 2 Oct — and no 21 Sep march appears in any source.
   OUTCOME: Bayrou's government dissolved; the 2023 pension reform put on hold. */
const BLOQUONS: Episode = {
  id: "bt",
  start: "2025-09-10",
  end: "2025-10-05",
  weeks: [
    {
      w: 0,
      from: "2025-09-08",
      to: "2025-09-14",
      p: [175_000, 250_000],
      a: 540,
      flag: { p: "c" },
    },
    {
      w: 1,
      from: "2025-09-15",
      to: "2025-09-21",
      p: [500_000, 1_000_000],
      a: 309,
      f: 26,
      i: 11,
    },
    { w: 3, from: "2025-09-29", to: "2025-10-05", p: [195_000, 600_000] },
  ],
};

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- 2026 rows */

/** STUDENT_WEEKS already carries bands, flags and note refs; this is a copy, not
 *  a recalculation — the fallback table and the index patch are gone.
 *  The window now opens on the week of 14 Sep 2026, so the episode runs nine
 *  weekly rows instead of seven:
 *    w0 14–20 Sep — no published figure for any metric (EMPTY)
 *    w1 21–27 Sep — 21 interpellations (Paris only, "r")
 *    w2 28 Sep–4 Oct — Act I (29 Sep) and Acte II (1 Oct); the week's injury
 *        figure is the cumulative level of 235 reached on 1 Oct ("c")
 *    w3 5–11 Oct — Acte III (6 Oct)
 *    w4 12–18 Oct — 17 Oct
 *    w5 19–25 Oct — 24 Oct
 *    w6 26 Oct–1 Nov — 31 Oct
 *    w7 2–8 Nov — 5 and 7 Nov
 *    w8 9–15 Nov — 9 Nov, clipped by the window
 *  Week 0's injury cell is empty, and the first injury figure is the floor
 *  of 1 on 28 Sep; 17 Oct (flag "e") anchors the injury series. */
function rows2026(): Row[] {
  return STUDENT_WEEKS.map((wk: StudentWeek, w) => {
    const row: Row = { w, from: wk.key, to: wk.end, partial: wk.touchesEdge };
    for (const m of METRICS) {
      const band = wk[m];
      if (band) row[m] = band;
      const flag = wk.flags[m];
      if (flag && flag !== "n") (row.flag ??= {})[m] = flag;
      const ref = wk.notes[m];
      if (ref) (row.noteRef ??= {})[m] = ref;
    }
    return row;
  });
}

const STUDENTS: Episode = {
  id: "students26",
  start: STUDENT_WEEKS[0].key,
  end: STUDENT_WEEKS[STUDENT_WEEKS.length - 1].end,
  weeks: rows2026(),
};

const EPISODES: readonly Episode[] = [
  MAY68,
  CPE,
  GILETS,
  NAHEL,
  PENSIONS,
  BLOQUONS,
  STUDENTS,
];

/** The cap for the companion trellis is the largest week INDEX at which any
 *  episode carries an arrest or injury figure — not the latest date.
 *  That is w11 (6 Apr 2023, pensions: 111 arrests, 154 police injured).
 *  The 2026 episode now runs its enforcement from w2 (28 Sep) to w8 (9 Nov). */
const ENFORCEMENT_LAST = EPISODES.reduce(
  (last, ep) =>
    ep.weeks.reduce(
      (l, r) =>
        r.a !== undefined || r.f !== undefined || r.i !== undefined
          ? Math.max(l, r.w)
          : l,
      last,
    ),
  0,
);

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------- text */

/** Tooltip title: "16 – 22 Jan · 19 Jan", "Act 1 · 17 Nov 2018", "W3 · 12–18 Oct". */
function rowTitle(ep: Episode, r: Row): string {
  const m = t();
  if (ep.id === "students26") {
    const base = fmtWeek(r.from, r.to);
    return r.partial ? fill(m.panels.partialWeek, { label: base }) : base;
  }
  const when =
    r.act !== undefined
      ? fill(m.panels.act, { n: r.act })
      : fmtWeek(r.from, r.to);
  const action = m.episodes[ep.id].weeks[r.w]?.action;
  return action ? fill(m.panels.withAction, { when, action }) : when;
}

function noteFor(id: EpisodeId, r: Row, m: Metric): string | undefined {
  if (id === "students26") {
    return (
      noteText(r.noteRef?.[m]) || t().episodes.students26.fallbackNote?.[m]
    );
  }
  return t().episodes[id].weeks[r.w]?.note?.[m];
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- indexing */

/** Memoised: rawExtent() used to rebuild every series ~33× per render, each pass
 *  re-running t(), fmtWeek() and noteText(). Cleared on every mount (locale-bound). */
let seriesCache = new Map<string, Pt[] | null>();

function seriesData(ep: Episode, key: Metric): Pt[] | null {
  const id = `${ep.id}:${key}`;
  if (seriesCache.has(id)) return seriesCache.get(id)!;

  const rows = ep.weeks
    .filter((r) => r[key] !== undefined)
    .sort((a, b) => a.w - b.w);
  const out =
    rows.length === 0
      ? null
      : (() => {
          const flagOf = (r: Row): Flag => r.flag?.[key] ?? "n";
          const baseRow = rows.find((r) => flagOf(r) !== "c") ?? rows[0];
          const base = midOf(baseRow[key]!);
          return rows.map((r) => {
            const [low, high] = bandOf(r[key]!);
            return {
              x: r.w,
              y: (low + high) / 2,
              low,
              high,
              flag: flagOf(r),
              note: noteFor(ep.id, r, key),
              base,
              t: rowTitle(ep, r),
            };
          });
        })();

  seriesCache.set(id, out);
  return out;
}

function buildDataset(
  ep: Episode,
  epIndex: number,
  key: Metric,
  color: string,
  maxX: number,
): PanelDataset | null {
  const all = seriesData(ep, key);
  if (!all) return null;

  const points = all.filter((p) => p.x <= maxX);
  if (!points.length) return null;

  return {
    label: t().items[key].label,
    data: points,
    borderColor: color,
    backgroundColor: color,
    borderWidth: 1.75,
    pointRadius: points.map((p) => (p.flag === "c" ? 3 : 2.75)),
    pointBackgroundColor: points.map((p) =>
      isDetached(p.flag) ? THEME.hollow : color,
    ),
    pointBorderColor: color,
    pointBorderWidth: 1.5,
    pointHoverRadius: 4.5,
    pointHitRadius: 12,
    tension: 0,
    spanGaps: true,
    segment: gapSegment(1, [5, 4]),
    __item: key,
    __ep: epIndex,
    __color: color,
  };
}

function rawExtent(
  eps: readonly Episode[],
  keys: readonly Metric[],
  maxX: number,
): [number, number] {
  let lo = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  let hi = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;

  for (const ep of eps) {
    for (const k of keys) {
      const pts = seriesData(ep, k);
      if (!pts) continue;
      for (const p of pts) {
        if (p.x > maxX) continue;
        if (p.low > 0) lo = Math.min(lo, p.low);
        hi = Math.max(hi, p.high);
      }
    }
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(lo) || !Number.isFinite(hi) || lo <= 0 || hi <= lo)
    return [1, 1e6];
  return [lo, hi];
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ scales */

function xScale(maxW: number) {
  const step = maxW <= 8 ? 1 : maxW <= 12 ? 2 : maxW <= 24 ? 4 : 8;
  return {
    type: "linear" as const,
    min: -0.5,
    max: maxW + 0.5,
    grid: { display: false },
    border: { display: false },
    ticks: {
      autoSkip: false,
      maxRotation: 0,
      font: { size: 9 },
      color: THEME.muted,
      padding: 4,
      callback: (v: string | number) => String(Number(v)),
    },
    afterBuildTicks: (axis: { ticks: { value: number }[] }) => {
      const out: { value: number }[] = [];
      for (let v = 0; v <= maxW + 0.001; v += step) out.push({ value: v });
      axis.ticks = out;
    },
    afterFit: (s: { height: number }) => {
      s.height = 20;
    },
  };
}

/** Padding in log space keeps every marker off the frame without inventing round bounds. */
function logBounds(lo: number, hi: number): [number, number] {
  const l = Math.log10(Math.max(lo, 1e-6));
  const h = Math.log10(Math.max(hi, l + 0.1));
  const pad = Math.max(0.06, (h - l) * 0.05);
  return [10 ** (l - pad), 10 ** (h + pad)];
}

/** 1-per-decade for wide ranges, 1-and-3 for medium, 1-2-5 for narrow. */
function logTicks(min: number, max: number): number[] {
  const l = Math.log10(min);
  const span = Math.log10(max) - l;
  const mantissas = span > 4.5 ? [1] : span > 1.5 ? [1, 3] : [1, 2, 5];
  const out: number[] = [];

  for (let e = Math.floor(l); e <= Math.ceil(Math.log10(max)); e++) {
    for (const mm of mantissas) {
      const v = Number((mm * 10 ** e).toPrecision(12));
      if (v > min * 1.01 && v < max / 1.01) out.push(v);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function yScale(min: number, max: number, showLabels: boolean) {
  const ticks = logTicks(min, max);
  return {
    type: "logarithmic" as const,
    min,
    max,
    grid: { color: THEME.grid, drawTicks: false, lineWidth: 1 },
    border: { display: false },
    ticks: {
      display: showLabels,
      font: { size: 9 },
      color: THEME.muted,
      padding: 6,
      callback: (v: string | number) => compact().format(Number(v)),
    },
    afterBuildTicks: (axis: { ticks: { value: number }[] }) => {
      axis.ticks = ticks.map((value) => ({ value }));
    },
    afterFit: (s: { width: number }) => {
      s.width = 46;
    },
  };
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- tooltip */

const pctOf = (v: number, base: number): string => {
  const r = (100 * v) / base;
  return r >= 10 ? `${Math.round(r)}%` : `${Number(r.toFixed(1))}%`;
};

function tooltipSpec() {
  const m = t();
  return {
    displayColors: false,
    callbacks: {
      title: (items: TooltipItem<"line">[]) => (items[0].raw as Pt).t,
      label: (item: TooltipItem<"line">) => {
        const p = item.raw as Pt;
        const value =
          p.low === p.high
            ? nf().format(Math.round(p.low))
            : `${nf().format(Math.round(p.low))} – ${nf().format(Math.round(p.high))}`;
        const flag = m.flags[p.flag];
        return flag
          ? fill(m.tooltip.lineWithFlag, { value, flag })
          : fill(m.tooltip.linePlain, { value });
      },
      afterLabel: (item: TooltipItem<"line">) => (item.raw as Pt).note ?? "",
      footer: (items: TooltipItem<"line">[]) => {
        const p = items[0].raw as Pt;
        const n = nf().format(Math.round(p.base));
        return Math.abs(p.y - p.base) / p.base < 1e-9
          ? fill(m.tooltip.firstFigure, { n })
          : fill(m.tooltip.shareOfFirst, { pct: pctOf(p.y, p.base), n });
      },
    },
  };
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- emphasis */

const episodePanels: Chart<"line">[] = [];
const itemPanels: { chart: Chart<"line">; item: Metric }[] = [];
let activeItem: Metric | null = null;
let activeEvent: number | null = null;

const datasetsOf = (chart: Chart<"line">): PanelDataset[] =>
  chart.data.datasets as PanelDataset[];

/** One recolour path for both emphasis modes. Hollow points are decided from the
 *  point's own flag at draw time, so `__hollow` no longer has to be kept in sync. */
function restyle(d: PanelDataset, on: boolean, bold = false): void {
  const c = on ? d.__color : alpha(d.__color, DIM);
  d.borderColor = c;
  d.pointBorderColor = c;
  d.pointBackgroundColor = d.data.map((p) =>
    isDetached(p.flag) ? THEME.hollow : c,
  );
  d.borderWidth = bold && on ? 2.75 : 1.75;
}

function restylePanels(
  charts: readonly Chart<"line">[],
  keep: (d: PanelDataset) => boolean,
  bold = false,
): void {
  for (const chart of charts) {
    for (const d of datasetsOf(chart)) restyle(d, keep(d), bold);
    chart.update("none");
  }
}

function emphasiseItem(key: Metric | null): void {
  if (key === activeItem) return;
  activeItem = key;
  restylePanels(episodePanels, (d) => key === null || d.__item === key, true);
  for (const { chart, item } of itemPanels) {
    const host = chart.canvas.parentElement;
    if (host)
      host.style.opacity = key === null || item === key ? "1" : String(DIM);
  }
}

function emphasiseEvent(index: number | null): void {
  if (index === activeEvent) return;
  activeEvent = index;
  restylePanels(
    itemPanels.map((p) => p.chart),
    (d) => index === null || d.__ep === index,
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- legends */

interface LegendEntry {
  text: string;
  color: string;
  on: () => void;
  off: () => void;
}

/** One builder for both legends; `pointerdown` makes the highlight reachable on touch. */
function legend(host: HTMLElement, entries: readonly LegendEntry[]): void {
  host.replaceChildren(
    ...entries.map(({ text, color, on, off }) => {
      const button = el("button");
      button.type = "button";
      const swatch = el("span", "swatch");
      swatch.style.background = color;
      button.append(swatch, document.createTextNode(text));
      for (const ev of ["mouseenter", "focus", "pointerdown"] as const)
        button.addEventListener(ev, on);
      for (const ev of ["mouseleave", "blur"] as const)
        button.addEventListener(ev, off);
      return button;
    }),
  );
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- panels */

function observations(ep: Episode): string {
  const m = t();
  const counts = METRICS.map(
    (k) => ep.weeks.filter((r) => r[k] !== undefined).length,
  ).join("·");
  return fill(m.panels.obs, {
    counts,
    weeks: plural(m.units.weeks, ep.weeks.length),
  });
}

function panelFigure(
  strip: string,
  meta: string,
  note?: string,
): { figure: HTMLElement; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement } {
  const figure = el("figure", "trellis-panel");
  const caption = el("figcaption");
  caption.append(el("span", "strip", strip), el("span", "strip-meta", meta));
  if (note) caption.append(el("p", "strip-note", note));

  const box = el("div", "panel-canvas");
  const canvas = el("canvas");
  box.append(canvas);
  figure.append(caption, box);
  return { figure, canvas };
}

function columns(host: HTMLElement): number {
  const w = host.clientWidth || 1200;
  return w >= 1180 ? 4 : w >= 620 ? 2 : 1;
}

/** One options factory for both trellises. */
const panelOptions = (
  x: object,
  y: object,
  onHover?: ChartOptions<"line">["onHover"],
): ChartOptions<"line"> => ({
  responsive: true,
  maintainAspectRatio: false,
  animation: false,
  interaction: { mode: "nearest", axis: "x", intersect: false },
  layout: { padding: { top: 6, right: 10, bottom: 0, left: 0 } },
  scales: { x, y } as ChartOptions<"line">["scales"],
  plugins: {
    legend: { display: false },
    title: { display: false },
    subtitle: { display: false },
    tooltip: tooltipSpec(),
  },
  onHover,
});

export interface TrellisHosts {
  episodeHost: HTMLElement;
  itemHost: HTMLElement;
  itemLegend: HTMLElement;
  eventLegend: HTMLElement;
}

export function mountCrossEpisode(opts: TrellisHosts): void {
  /* destroyAll() has already destroyed the previous instances (canvas.ts owns
     them); clear the handles so emphasis cannot touch a dead chart. */
  episodePanels.length = 0;
  itemPanels.length = 0;
  activeItem = null;
  activeEvent = null;
  seriesCache = new Map(); // titles and notes are locale-bound
  opts.episodeHost.replaceChildren();
  opts.itemHost.replaceChildren();

  const m = t();
  const unbounded = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

  /* ---- primary trellis: one panel per episode, one y range for all ---- */
  const [yMin, yMax] = logBounds(...rawExtent(EPISODES, METRICS, unbounded));
  const cols = columns(opts.episodeHost);
  opts.episodeHost.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${cols}, minmax(0, 1fr))`;

  EPISODES.forEach((ep, idx) => {
    const text = m.episodes[ep.id];
    const maxW = Math.max(...ep.weeks.map((r) => r.w), 1);
    const { figure, canvas } = panelFigure(
      text.strip,
      `${fmtSpan(ep.start, ep.end)} · ${observations(ep)}`,
      text.banner,
    );
    opts.episodeHost.append(figure);

    const datasets = SERIES.map((s) =>
      buildDataset(ep, idx, s.key, s.color, unbounded),
    ).filter((d): d is PanelDataset => d !== null);

    const chart = track(
      new Chart<"line">(canvas, {
        type: "line",
        data: { datasets } as unknown as ChartData<"line">,
        options: panelOptions(
          xScale(maxW),
          yScale(yMin, yMax, idx % cols === 0),
          (_e, elements) => {
            const d = elements[0]?.dataset as PanelDataset | undefined;
            emphasiseItem(d ? d.__item : null);
          },
        ),
        plugins: [], // no per-panel labels: the episode is the panel title
      }),
    );

    canvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => emphasiseItem(null));
    episodePanels.push(chart);
  });

  /* ---- companion trellis: one panel per series, x capped at the last enforcement week ---- */
  const itemCols = columns(opts.itemHost);
  opts.itemHost.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${itemCols}, minmax(0, 1fr))`;

  SERIES.forEach((series) => {
    const [rawLo, rawHi] = rawExtent(EPISODES, [series.key], ENFORCEMENT_LAST);
    const [lo, hi] = logBounds(rawLo, rawHi);
    const range = fill(m.panels.valueRange, {
      lo: compact().format(rawLo),
      hi: compact().format(rawHi),
    });

    const { figure, canvas } = panelFigure(
      m.items[series.key].short,
      fill(m.panels.itemMeta, { range, n: ENFORCEMENT_LAST }),
    );
    opts.itemHost.append(figure);

    const datasets = EPISODES.map((ep, i) =>
      buildDataset(ep, i, series.key, RAMP[i], ENFORCEMENT_LAST),
    ).filter((d): d is PanelDataset => d !== null);

    const chart = track(
      new Chart<"line">(canvas, {
        type: "line",
        data: { datasets } as unknown as ChartData<"line">,
        options: panelOptions(xScale(ENFORCEMENT_LAST), yScale(lo, hi, true)),
        plugins: [],
      }),
    );

    canvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => emphasiseEvent(null));
    canvas.addEventListener("mousemove", (ev) => {
      const pts = chart.getElementsAtEventForMode(
        ev,
        "nearest",
        { intersect: false },
        true,
      );
      if (!pts.length) {
        emphasiseEvent(null);
        return;
      }
      emphasiseEvent(datasetsOf(chart)[pts[0].datasetIndex].__ep);
    });

    itemPanels.push({ chart, item: series.key });
  });

  /* ---- legends ---- */
  legend(
    opts.itemLegend,
    SERIES.map((s) => ({
      text: m.items[s.key].label,
      color: s.color,
      on: () => emphasiseItem(s.key),
      off: () => emphasiseItem(null),
    })),
  );

  legend(
    opts.eventLegend,
    EPISODES.map((ep, i) => ({
      text: m.episodes[ep.id].short,
      color: RAMP[i],
      on: () => emphasiseEvent(i),
      off: () => emphasiseEvent(null),
    })),
  );
}
```

## Change log

| File | Change | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `data.ts` | `START` → `"2026-09-15"` | Window now opens at the Caen blockade |
| `data.ts` | New cells: 25 Sep (21 arrests, `r`), 28 Sep (164 arrests, 1 injury `f`), 29 Sep (turnout `p`, 440 arrests, 31 injuries `p`), 30 Sep (625 arrests, 119 injuries `c`), 2 Oct (1,747), 3 Oct (150 `e`, derived weekend) | First fortnight of the movement |
| `data.ts` | 1 Oct arrests `q: "c"` → `q: "n"` | 1,949 is that day's figure, not a stock [^2] |
| `data.ts` | 1 Oct forces 84–305 → 83–300 `e`; 1 Oct injuries 119–235 → 235 `c`; 30 Sep injuries 119 `c` | Two vintages of one series split onto their own dates [^1] |
| `data.ts` | New cells 5 Oct (984, 65) and 6 Oct (488, 43) | Previously absent; forces-injured total → 1,664–2,130 |
| `data.ts` | `aggregate()` rewritten: a week whose last cell is a stock shows that stock | The week of 28 Sep now reports the 235 cumulative injury level instead of the 32-day-flow sum |
| `data.ts` | `STUDENT_WEEKS` no longer attaches a flag to an empty week | The override could sprout a phantom marker on w0 |
| `episodes.ts` | 2026 episode now spans 14 Sep – 15 Nov, nine rows | Auto-derived from `STUDENT_WEEKS` |
| `episodes.ts` | PENSIONS gains w9 (457 arrests, 441 forces) and w11 (111, 154); w10 end `2026-04-02` → `2023-04-02`; w20 end → `2023-06-08` | Enforcement gap closed; two date typos fixed |
| `episodes.ts` | NAHEL p band → [8,000, 50,000] `e`; GJ_EXTRA replaced by sourced per-act figures; BLOQUONS w2 documented as intentionally empty | Carried over from the previous pass |

If `notes.ts` resolves those from a date-keyed table, the new dates will fall through to the fallback text. I could not check the file — it is not in this workspace |

The table now takes the reading that reproduces the ministry's own arithmetic (4,925 by Friday, 6,059 by Monday), and the weekend of 3–4 October is derived at 150 arrests. If you would rather not publish a derived weekend cell, delete the 3 October entry — the weekly total then reads 4,925 instead of 5,075 |

## What the comparison turned up

| # | Finding | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `i18n.ts` says **"Paris only"** for flag `p`, but `p` is *partial* in the flag vocabulary | The 29 Sep turnout (a public-service cortège count) would be labelled "Paris only" — wrong. `p` → "partial", and the Paris-only cells move to `r` |
| 2 | `obsNotes` for 10-20 and 11-09 say "Prefecture of police of Paris figure only" | Both are **regional** (Guadeloupe; Bordeaux/Lyon/Strasbourg). The copy was copy-pasted and was never right |
| 3 | My previous `data.ts` header claimed the policing total was "€49.2M–€484.9M — unchanged" | **It was not unchanged.** The September deployment estimates plus the missing 18 Oct curfew day move it to **€54.3M–€629.9M**. The old figure is withdrawn |
| 4 | The old aggregator summed the 6 Oct and 8 Oct damage cells (6 + 6 = €12M) | They are the *same* Île-de-France increment. The rewrite excludes the subset and reports €6M |
| 5 | `notes.ts` resolves only `obsNotes` by date+metric | The weekly points inherited "6 days counted" even when the week is a **stock** — now the stock's own note is reused |

## `notes.ts` (complete)

```ts
/* Note and marker copy. Lives apart from chart.ts because section C renders the
   same NoteRefs: that import was what pulled STUDENT_WEEKS into the wrong module.

   ── Third pass, 12 Nov 2026 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   Two kinds of note now share the same table:

   1. DAILY points carry { k: "obs", date, metric }. The text lives in
      i18n.ts under obsNotes[date][metric].
   2. WEEKLY points normally carry { k: "week", p }, rendered from the
      generic week copy ("N days counted", "includes a floor", "N points
      excluded"). But when a week is a cumulative stock, or when it contains a
      derived component that makes the figure uncertain, the weekly point
      borrows the OBSERVATION note of the cell that explains it — a stock is
      a level, not a sum, and "6 days counted" would be the wrong caption for
      it. The obsNotes entry therefore has to read correctly in both a daily
      and a weekly tooltip.

   Nothing here needs to know which context it is in: both resolve through
   the same { k: "obs" } branch. */

import { fill, fmtDayMonth, nf, t } from "./i18n.ts";
import { RATE } from "./data.ts";
import type { Flag, NoteRef } from "./model.ts";

/** Resolve a stored note reference into copy, in the active locale. */
export function noteText(ref: NoteRef | null | undefined): string {
  if (!ref) return "";
  const m = t().chart;

  switch (ref.k) {
    case "obs":
      return m.obsNotes[ref.date]?.[ref.metric] ?? "";

    case "policing": {
      const head =
        ref.low === ref.high
          ? nf().format(ref.low)
          : `${nf().format(ref.low)}–${nf().format(ref.high)}`;
      const note = m.deployNotes[ref.date];
      return fill(note ? m.policingDay : m.policingDayPlain, {
        head,
        low: nf().format(RATE.low),
        high: nf().format(RATE.high),
        note: note ?? "",
      });
    }

    case "cumulative":
      return fill(ref.added ? m.cumulativeAdded : m.cumulativeCarried, {
        days: ref.days,
      });

    case "week": {
      const p = ref.p;
      const parts = [
        fill(p.kind === "national" ? m.weekNational : m.weekRegionalOnly, {
          n: p.days,
        }),
      ];
      if (p.peak) parts.push(fill(m.weekPeak, { date: fmtDayMonth(p.peak) }));
      if (p.noNational) parts.push(m.weekNoNational);
      if (p.excluded) parts.push(fill(m.weekExcluded, { n: p.excluded }));
      if (p.floored) parts.push(m.weekFloored);
      if (p.clipped) parts.push(m.weekClipped);
      return parts.join(m.weekJoin);
    }

    default:
      return "";
  }
}

/** Marker suffix for a tooltip value. `detached` wins only when no flag applies. */
export function suffixFor(
  q: Flag | null | undefined,
  detached: boolean,
): string {
  const s = t().chart.suffix;
  const text =
    q === "c"
      ? s.c
      : q === "w"
        ? s.w
        : q === "e"
          ? s.e
          : q === "f"
            ? s.f
            : detached
              ? s.regional
              : "";
  return text ? fill(t().chart.suffixWrap, { text }) : "";
}
```

```ts
const EN_META: LocaleMeta = {
  tag: "en-GB",
  label: "English",
  meta: {
    htmlLang: "en",
    title: "Protest activity in France — September–November 2026",
    description:
      "Turnout, arrests, injuries and damage in the autumn 2026 movement, set against six earlier French movements.",
  },
  sections: {
    a: {
      title: "A · Daily counts, 15 September – 11 November 2026",
      blurb:
        "One marker per day on which something was published, from the first mainland high-school blockades on 15 September. Days with no figure stay empty rather than interpolated; gaps in a line are dashed.",
    },
    b: {
      title: "B · Weekly aggregates",
      blurb:
        "Weeks run Monday to Sunday, from the week of 14 September. A peak-day crowd and a summed weekly arrest count are not the same statistic — the tooltip says which is which.",
    },
    c: {
      title: "C · One panel per movement",
      blurb:
        "Each panel keeps its own time span on a shared logarithmic axis, so the shapes are comparable but the durations are not.",
      note: "Hollow markers are regional, Paris-only, modelled or stock figures. Dashed connectors span a week with nothing published.",
    },
    cp: {
      title: "C′ · Transposed: one panel per series",
      blurb:
        "The same data with movements overlaid, so a single measure can be read across 1968 to 2026. The x axis is elapsed weeks, capped at the last week with enforcement figures.",
      note: "Hover a measure or a movement to isolate it. Panel order matches legend order.",
    },
  },
};
```

```ts
    },
    b: {
      title: "B · Agrégats hebdomadaires",

    c: {
      title: "C · Un panneau par mouvement",

    cp: {
      title: "C′ · Transposé : un panneau par série",
      blurb:
        "Les mêmes données avec les mouvements superposés, pour lire une même mesure de 1968 à 2026. L’axe des x est le nombre de semaines écoulées, arrêté à la dernière semaine de chiffres de maintien de l’ordre.",
      note: "Survolez une mesure ou un mouvement pour l’isoler. L’ordre des panneaux suit celui de la légende.",
    },
  },
};
```

```ts
   ── Third pass, 12 Nov 2026: the catalogue catches up with the window ───
   FIX 9:  the policing-cost total was mis-stated in the second pass. The
           September and 2/5 October deployment estimates and the missing
           18 October curfew day move the movement total from
           €49.2M–€484.9M to €54.3M–€629.9M. The old figure was the
           pre-September total and is withdrawn.
   FIX 10: 18 October had a curfew and published Paris arrests but no
           deployment cell, so the cost series silently skipped the day.
           Added at 2,000–8,000 agents (Paris scale, flagged "e").
   FIX 11: the 18 October arrest cell is Paris-only, so it is "r" like the
           other regional cells. "p" now means partial coverage everywhere,
           matching the flag vocabulary.
   FIX 12: the weekly aggregator is rewritten. A week whose last cell is a
           cumulative level takes that level as its figure; a "c" cell that
           is a subset of a larger published total is excluded from sums; a
           week containing a derived component is explained by that
           component's own note. This is what stops the 6 and 8 October
           damage cells (the same €6M increment) being added twice.

   Arithmetic cross-checks:
     · arrest flows sum to 6,547 through 6 Oct = ministry stock 6,059 (5 Oct)
       + 488 (6 Oct) ✓;
     · weekly arrests: 21 · 5,075 · 1,472 · 2,397–2,628 · 1,314–1,435 ·

     · weekly forces injured: 108 · 1,037–1,184 · 267 · 116–159 · 53–112 ✓;
     · forces injured, movement total = 1,664–2,130;
     · policing cost = DEPLOYMENT × RATE = €54.3M–€629.9M.
```

**④ `aggregate()` — the rewrite**

Replace the whole function and its comment block:

```ts
/* ── Aggregation rules (third pass, 12 Nov 2026) ─────────────────────────
   1. A "c" cell is a cumulative level, or a subset of a larger published
      total. It is never added to a flow.
   2. If the week's LAST cell for the metric is a cumulative level, and
      nothing else in the week is larger, that level IS the week's figure —
      a level is a stronger statement than a partial sum, and summing flows
      into it would double-count. The week is flagged "c" and detached, and
      it borrows the cell's own observation note, because "N days counted"
      would be the wrong caption for a stock.
   3. Otherwise a "c" cell that is a subset of a bigger figure in the same
      week is excluded from the sum and counted in the note. This is what
      keeps the 6 and 8 October damage cells (one €6M increment, published
      twice) from being added to €12M.
   4. Regional-only ("r") cells are excluded whenever a national-scope cell
      exists for the same week.
   5. A week containing a derived ("e") component is explained by that
      component's own note, so the tooltip says what makes the figure
      uncertain.
   6. The weekly figure is a weekly aggregate ("w") unless the week is
      regional only ("r"), includes a floor ("f") or is partial ("p").     */
function aggregate(key: MetricKey, op: "sum" | "max"): Series {
  return WEEKS.map((week) => {
    const cells: { date: string; obs: Obs; q: Flag }[] = [];
    for (const d of week.dates) {
      const obs = FACTS[d]?.[key];
      if (!obs) continue;
      cells.push({ date: d, obs, q: obs.q ?? "n" });
    }
    if (!cells.length) return NIL;

    const last = cells[cells.length - 1];
    const othersHigh = Math.max(
      ...cells
        .filter((c) => c !== last)
        .map((c) => c.obs.high),
      Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY,
    );

    /* Rule 2 — the week's last cell is a cumulative level and nothing in
       the week is larger: the level IS the week's figure. */
    if (last.q === "c" && last.obs.high >= othersHigh) {
      return {
        v: round2((last.obs.low + last.obs.high) / 2),
        band: [round2(last.obs.low), round2(last.obs.high)] as [number, number],
        q: "c",
        note: { k: "obs", date: last.date, metric: key } as NoteRef,
        detach: isDetached("c"),
      };
    }

    /* Rules 3–4 — split the cells. */
    const regional = cells.filter((c) => c.q === "r");
    const subsets = cells.filter((c) => c.q === "c");
    const flows = cells.filter((c) => c.q !== "c" && c.q !== "r");
    const regionalOnly = flows.length === 0 && regional.length > 0;
    let pool = regionalOnly ? regional : flows;

    /* Nothing but subsets in the week: take the largest published stock. */
    if (!pool.length) {
      if (!subsets.length) return NIL;
      pool = [
        subsets.reduce((best, x) => (x.obs.high > best.obs.high ? x : best)),
      ];
    }

    let low: number;
    let high: number;
    let value: number;
    let peak: string | undefined;

    if (op === "sum") {
      low = pool.reduce((a, x) => a + x.obs.low, 0);
      high = pool.reduce((a, x) => a + x.obs.high, 0);
      value = (low + high) / 2;
    } else {
      low = Math.max(...pool.map((x) => x.obs.low));
      high = Math.max(...pool.map((x) => x.obs.high));
      const top = pool.reduce((best, x) =>
        (x.obs.low + x.obs.high) / 2 > (best.obs.low + best.obs.high) / 2
          ? x
          : best,
      );
      value = (top.obs.low + top.obs.high) / 2;
      peak = top.date;
    }

    /* Rule 5 — a derived component explains the week better than the
       generic note. */
    const derived = pool
      .filter((x) => x.q === "e")
      .sort((a, b) => b.obs.high - a.obs.high)[0];

    const excluded = cells.length - pool.length;
    const partial = pool.some((x) => x.q === "p");
    const parts: WeekNoteParts = {
      days: new Set(pool.map((x) => x.date)).size,
      kind: regionalOnly ? "regional" : "national",
      peak,
      noNational: regionalOnly || undefined,
      excluded: excluded > 0 ? excluded : undefined,
      floored: pool.some((x) => x.q === "f") || undefined,
      clipped: week.touchesEdge || undefined,
    };

    return {
      v: round2(value),
      band: [round2(low), round2(high)] as [number, number],
      q: regionalOnly ? "r" : parts.floored ? "f" : partial ? "p" : "w",
      note: derived
        ? ({ k: "obs", date: derived.date, metric: key } as NoteRef)
        : ({ k: "week", p: parts } as NoteRef),
      detach: regionalOnly,
    };
  });
}
```
